### [1] [Hidden Supersymmetry in Wigner-Yang Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04503)
*Georg Junker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究实直线上的量子系统，该系统遵循Wigner于1950年提出的变形海森堡代数。其坐标表示由Yang在1951年给出，本质上与Dunkl在1989年引入的差-微分算子形式相同。在特定条件下，该量子系统表现出超对称（SUSY）结构，其中反射算子充当分级算子。作者通过首先仅考虑相空间中两个运动方程中的一个，给出了Yang表示的一般化推广，发现对应的非相互作用系统代表Witten的SUSY量子力学模型。再施加第二个运动方程，将Wigner-Yang的原始结果推广到实直线上的一般对称势。以谐振子和吸引型类库仑势V(x)=-γ/|x|为显式例子进行讨论，并建立了该库仑系统与Wigner-Yang谐振子系统之间的Hooke-Newton对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Wigner-Yang变形海森堡代数的原始工作，将其从谐振子特例推广到一般对称势，并揭示该代数与Dunkl算子及超对称量子力学之间的联系，从而统一多个看起来不相关的物理和数学结构。

Method: 作者采用分步策略：首先仅施加相空间中两个运动方程之一，得到与Witten SUSY量子力学模型等价的非相互作用系统；然后施加第二个运动方程，将Wigner-Yang的结果推广至一般对称势。利用反射算子作为超对称的分级算子，分别对谐振子和吸引型类库仑势V(x)=-γ/|x|进行显式求解，并建立两者间的Hooke-Newton对偶映射。

Result: 给出了Yang表示的推广形式，证明了一阶方程对应的系统等价于Witten的SUSY量子力学模型；将Wigner-Yang的原始构造推广到一般对称势；对谐振子和类库仑势两种系统进行了显式分析，并建立了二者之间的Hooke-Newton对偶关系。

Conclusion: 变形海森堡代数（Wigner代数）与Dunkl算子、超对称量子力学之间存在深刻的数学联系；通过分阶段施加运动方程，可以系统地构建具有超对称结构的可解量子系统，并能通过Hooke-Newton对偶将谐振子与类库仑系统统一处理。

Abstract: We consider a quantum system on the real line obeying a deformed Heisenberg algebra originally proposed by Wigner in 1950. Its explicit coordinate representation was provided by Yang in 1951 and in essence is identical in form with Dunkl's difference-differential operator introduced in 1989 in connection with roots systems of refection groups. Under certain conditions such quantum systems exhibit a supersymmetric (SUSY) structure where the reflection operator acts as the grading operator. We present a generalisation of Yang's representation by first considering only of one the two equations of motion in phase space. The corresponding non-interacting system is found to represent Witten's model of SUSY quantum mechanics. Imposing also the second equation of motion the original result of Wigner and Yang is reconsidered by extending their discussion to general symmetric potentials on the real line. As explicit example we discuss the harmonic oscillator and an attractive Coulomb-like potential $V(x)=-γ/|x|$. We also establish a Hooke-Newton duality between this Coulomb-like system and the original Wigner-Yang harmonic oscillator system.

</details>


### [2] [Multi-frequency far-field data enrichment for electromagnetic source reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04829)
*Atyab Khalifa Al-Shaqsi,Heba Mohammed Al-Subhi,Xianchao Wang,Shujaat Khan,Abdul Wahab*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段电磁源重建策略，利用紧凑支撑、几何稀疏源具有有限率创新（FRI）的物理特性，通过构造环绕结构Hankel矩阵并利用其低秩性进行矩阵补全（ALOHA方法）来丰富欠采样数据，然后通过Fourier反演从富化数据中重建电流源密度，有效解决了欠采样引起的非唯一性和伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从远场辐射模式重建未知电磁源是基础逆问题，但在实际中按奈奎斯特采样率密集采集多频远场数据往往不可行。欠采样或稀疏数据会引入非辐射源分量，破坏解的唯一性，导致标准反演技术产生严重伪影。

Method: 两阶段重建策略。第一阶段：利用紧凑支撑、几何稀疏源具有有限率创新（FRI）的物理性质，构造环绕结构Hankel矩阵，利用其低秩性将多频远场数据的缺失恢复转化为约束矩阵补全问题，通过基于湮灭滤波器的低秩Hankel矩阵补全方法（ALOHA）求解。第二阶段：通过Fourier反演方案从丰富化后的数据集重建电流源密度。

Result: 大量电磁源模型的数值评估表明，该框架有效消除欠采样伪影并解决非唯一性挑战。在30%至50%的可用样本高欠采样率和10 dB信噪比的强噪声条件下，仍能实现准确且稳定的重建，优于标准ℓ1压缩感知基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的FRI低秩矩阵补全驱动的数据丰富化框架能有效克服远场欠采样带来的逆问题病态性，在极端欠采样和噪声条件下实现稳定、准确的电磁源重建，为实际应用中稀疏测量场景下的源反演提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing unknown electromagnetic sources from far-field radiation patterns is a fundamental inverse problem with broad applications in biomedical imaging, non-destructive testing, and telecommunications. In practical settings, however, collecting dense multi-frequency far-field measurements at the Nyquist sampling rate is often infeasible. Under-sampled or sparse data introduce non-radiating source components that sever the uniqueness of the solution, creating severe artifacts when standard inversion techniques are applied. To overcome this limitation, we present a two-stage reconstruction strategy exploiting the physical property that compactly supported, geometrically sparse sources exhibit a finite rate of innovations (FRI). In the first stage, we construct an associated wrap-around structured Hankel matrix. By leveraging the low-rank property of the matrix due to FRI of the unknown sources, we enrich the sub-sampled data. To that end, we convert missing multi-frequency far-field data recovery into a constrained matrix completion task solved via Annihilating Filter-based Low-rank Hankel Matrix Completion Approach (ALOHA). In the second stage, a Fourier inversion scheme reconstructs the current source density from the enriched dataset. Extensive numerical evaluations on electromagnetic source models show that our enrichment framework effectively eliminates under-sampling artifacts and resolves non-uniqueness challenges. The method delivers accurate and stable reconstructions under high sub-sampling rates (e.g., with $30$\% to $50$\% available samples) and strong noise conditions ($10$ dB SNR), outperforming standard $\ell_1$-compressed sensing baselines.

</details>


### [3] [Phase transitions in generalized XY models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04972)
*Fabio Plaga*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类广义二维XY模型的去局域化（delocalisation）可以排除向列序参量的指数衰减，通过广义圈表示建立了高度差方差与向列序参量的联系，推广了van Engelenburg和Lis的结果，并利用Pfister的比较论证恢复了向列区域的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将高度函数模型与广义二维XY模型建立联系，证明在广义XY模型中向列序参量不存在指数衰减（即存在向列序），从而推广之前van Engelenburg和Lis (2023)关于标准XY模型中径向序的结果。

Method: 构造广义圈表示（generalized loop representation），将两个面之间的高度差方差与向列序参量联系起来；再结合高度函数模型的去局域化性质证明向列序参量不能指数衰减；最后用Pfister (1982)的经典比较论证恢复向列区域的存在性。

Result: 对包含Korshunov以及Lee和Grinstein独立提出的广义XY模型在内的一类模型，证明了高度函数去局域化排除了向列序参量的指数衰减，并恢复了温度-耦合常数参数空间中向列区域的存在性。

Conclusion: 广义圈表示是连接高度函数模型与广义XY模型向列序的有力工具，此类方法可广泛应用于更广泛的自旋模型族，并推广了标准XY模型的已知结果。

Abstract: We associate height function models to a range of generalized two-dimensional XY models and prove that delocalisation of the height function model rules out exponential decay of the nematic order parameter in the primal spin model. The argument is based on a generalized loop representation which relates the variance of a height difference between two faces to the nematic order parameter. This generalizes the results by van Engelenburg and Lis (2023). The range of models also includes the much-studied generalized XY model as proposed by Korshunov and independently by Lee and Grinstein, where neighbouring spins interact ferromagnetically favouring parallel alignment and nematically favouring parallel or antiparallel alignment. Moreover, we recover the existence of a nematic region by a classical comparison argument of Pfister (1982).

</details>

### [4] [Disformal Maps: Classification and Singular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04094)
*Mohammad Ali Gorji,Pavel Jiroušek,Alexander Vikman,Masahide Yamaguchi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究四维洛伦兹流形上一般性disformal变换g_{μν}=Ch_{μν}+Dt_{μν}的数学结构。利用Cayley-Hamilton定理推导出逆disformal度规的显式公式，并通过Hawking-Ellis分类将disformal变换划分为四种类型及其Segre子类，建立了Cayley-Hamilton阶数、Hawking-Ellis类型与Segre子类之间的显式联系。对于奇异变换产生的mimetic能动张量T*_{μν}，分析了其与t_{μν}的Hawking-Ellis类型关系，并应用于标量场和规范场两种具体情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对引力系统场内容保持不可知论态度，为一般性disformal变换建立系统的数学分类框架，从而能够系统地研究各种可逆和不可逆disformal变换的运动学与动力学性质。

Method: 利用Cayley-Hamilton定理推导逆度规公式；采用Hawking-Ellis分类（基于Jordan块结构划分为Type I-IV）结合Segre符号进行精细子类划分；对奇异变换推导mimetic能动张量的通式并比较其与原始t_{μν}的Hawking-Ellis类型。

Result: 建立了Cayley-Hamilton阶数与Hawking-Ellis类型及Segre子类之间的显式关联；发现仅凭Cayley-Hamilton阶数即可约束或直接确定Hawking-Ellis类型（无需完整Jordan分解）。对于奇异变换，Type I的Hawking-Ellis类型在t_{μν}与T*_{μν}间始终一致，Type II/III可不同，Type IV通常保持但可退化至Type I；并将结果应用于标量场梯度张量和规范场强度张量两种具体情形。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统研究不同场内容下可逆与不可逆disformal变换提供了统一数学工具，允许直接从t_{μν}的Hawking-Ellis类型推断T*_{μν}的物理性质。

Abstract: Being agnostic about the field content of a gravitational system, we consider a general disformal transformation of the metric, $g_{μν}=Ch_{μν}+Dt_{μν}$, on a four-dimensional Lorentzian manifold. Using the Cayley-Hamilton theorem, we derive an explicit formula for the inverse disformed metric. Implementing the Hawking-Ellis classification, we categorize disformal transformations into four types: Type I, II, III, and IV, based on possible Jordan block structures. By examining the eigenvalues, we further classify each type into its corresponding Segre subclasses. We find explicit links between the Cayley-Hamilton degree of the disformal tensor $t_{μν}$, its Hawking-Ellis type, and its Segre subclass, which can restrict the possible Hawking-Ellis types once only the Cayley-Hamilton degree is known. In some cases, the type can be determined without even performing a full Jordan decomposition. For singular transformations, when new dynamical degrees of freedom emerge, we obtain the general form of their corresponding mimetic energy-momentum tensor $T^\star_{μν}$. We show that the Hawking-Ellis types of $t_{μν}$ and $T^\star_{μν}$ always coincide for Type I. For Types II and III it can differ, while Type IV is preserved generically but can reduce to Type I when the complex pair is mapped to a repeated real eigenvalue. This makes it possible to infer physical properties of $T^\star_{μν}$ directly from the Hawking-Ellis type of $t_{μν}$. We apply our setup to two specific cases: $t_{μν}=\partial_μφ\partial_νφ$ and $t_{μν}=F^α{}_μF_{αν}$, where $φ$ is a scalar field and $F_{μν}$ is the field-strength tensor of a gauge field. This general framework can be used to systematically study the kinematical and dynamical properties of various invertible and non-invertible disformal transformations with different field content.

</details>


### [5] [Corrections to Hawking radiation from asteroid-mass primordial black holes: analytic and numerical evaluation of the stochastic charge effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04346)
*Gabriel Vasquez,Bowen Chen,Cara Nel,Emily Koivu,Makana Silva,Christopher M. Hirata*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文是系列论文的第二篇，通过在Schwarzschild时空上的量子电动力学（QED）框架下，首次严格推导了原始黑洞（PBH）蒸发过程中电子和正电子的随机电荷效应（stochastic charge effect）。研究发现，在2.2×10^16 - 1.7×10^17 g的质量范围内，对e±发射率的净修正小于2%，远低于此前半经典方法预测的5%抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 霍金辐射对M~10^16 g量级的原始黑洞作为暗物质候选者施加了严格的约束，这需要精确建模光子、电子和正电子的发射谱。此前Page在1977年通过半经典论证预测了"随机电荷效应"，但缺乏严格的QED推导，需要从量子电动力学层面验证该效应。

Method: 在Schwarzschild时空上构建QED框架，从哈密顿量中提取相关项（长程单极子项H_int,0L），对精细结构常数α做一阶微扰展开，推导e±发射的修正项。系统对比了QED计算与半经典结果，识别了仅存在于量子力学方法中的新项。

Result: QED计算确认了半经典项的存在，但由于若干项的相互抵消，在2.2×10^16 - 1.7×10^17 g质量范围内，对e±发射率的净修正小于2%，而此前半经典论证预测的抑制高达5%。此外还指出了仅在量子力学方法中出现的项。

Conclusion: 随机电荷效应可以在QED框架下从第一性原理推导，但半经典方法高估了其对e±发射率的抑制幅度。真实量子修正（<2%）小于此前预期（5%），这影响了对PBH暗物质候选者的蒸发产物的精确建模和相应约束的强度。

Abstract: Hawking radiation sets stringent constraints on Primordial Black Holes (PBHs) as a dark matter candidate in the $M\sim 10^{16}$ g regime based on the evaporation products produced by the black hole, motivating the need to rigorously model the photon, electron, and positron emission spectra. This manuscript is the second in a series of two papers (see Vasquez et al. [Phys. Rev. D, 112:063002 (2025)] for the first paper in the series) with the goal of proving the stochastic emission of electrons and positrons, known as the "stochastic charge effect" and first predicted by Page [Phys. Rev. D, 16:2402 (1977)] using semi-classical arguments, arises from quantum electrodynamics (QED) on a Schwarzschild spacetime. We derive the corrections to the $e^\pm$ from the relevant term in the Hamiltonian (the long-range monopole: $H_{\rm int,0L}$) to first order in the fine structure constant ($α$), and show that the semi-classical terms are also present in the QED calculation. We also highlight which terms in our analysis only appear within our quantum mechanical approach and cannot be explained otherwise. We find that due to some cancellation of terms, over the range of $2.2\times 10^{16}$ - $1.7\times 10^{17}$ g, the net correction to the $e^\pm$ emission rate is less than 2%, versus the suppression of up to 5% previously found by semi-classical arguments.

</details>


### [6] [Masking Black Hole Spin with a Modified Chaplygin Gas Envelope: Radiative Degeneracies from a Phenomenological Three-Region Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04438)
*Sandip Dutta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个理论框架，用Modified Chaplygin Gas（MCG）状态方程描述被有界暗物质包层包围的旋转致密天体，通过分段三区域TOV积分构建严格满足爱因斯坦场方程的时空，并结合压力修正的Kerr形式引入旋转。在该几何下求解赤道圆测地线和ISCO，并通过Novikov-Thorne形式分析薄吸积盘热力学，发现MCG包层的深引力势阱将辐射效率提升至约6.5%，且存在静态/慢旋转黑洞与致密MCG结构之间的结构简并性，该框架可用于量化黑洞自旋估计中的环境系统性不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视界尺度观测的理论解读通常假设孤立真空Kerr几何，但实际天体物理黑洞被暗物质分布包围，局部时空几何会受到影响。作者旨在构建一个更符合物理现实的框架，以量化环境效应对黑洞自旋估计的系统性偏差。

Method: 采用MCG状态方程描述有界暗物质包层；构造三区域分段时空，通过完全耦合的TOV积分严格满足爱因斯坦场方程，使流体压力自然衰减至零并动态确定外边界；旋转通过压力修正的Kerr形式引入，时间分量直接来自积分的流体静力学势；精确评估4D赤道矩阵行列式而非真空近似；求解赤道圆测地线确定ISCO，并用Novikov-Thorne形式评估薄吸积盘热力学。

Result: MCG包层的深引力势阱显著增强粘性耗散，系统性地将峰值热通量、有效温度和多色黑体光谱光度推向更高能段；发现结构简并性：嵌入致密MCG结构的静态或慢旋转黑洞可将辐射效率提升至η≈6.5%。

Conclusion: 该框架作为结构化方案提出，用于量化标准黑洞自旋估计技术中的环境系统性不确定度，揭示暗物质包层对吸积盘辐射特征和自旋测量可能产生的显著影响。

Abstract: Theoretical interpretations of horizon-scale observations often rely on the idealized assumption of an isolated vacuum Kerr geometry. However, astrophysical black holes are expected to be embedded within dense dark matter distributions that can modify the local spacetime geometry. In this work, we propose a theoretical framework to model a rotating compact object surrounded by a bounded dark matter envelope governed by a Modified Chaplygin Gas (MCG) equation of state. To ensure strict adherence to the Einstein Field Equations, we construct a piece-wise, three-region spacetime using a fully coupled Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff (TOV) integration, allowing the fluid's pressure to taper naturally to zero and dynamically define the outer boundary. Rotation is introduced via a pressure-corrected Kerr-form ansatz where the temporal component is obtained directly from the integrated hydrostatic potential. Using this geometrically rigorous configuration, which explicitly evaluates the exact 4D equatorial metric determinant rather than relying on vacuum approximations, we solve the circular equatorial geodesics and determine the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO). Evaluating the thin accretion disk thermodynamics via the Novikov-Thorne formalism reveals that the deep gravitational potential well of the MCG envelope acts as a strong driver for viscous dissipation, systematically shifting the peak thermal flux, effective temperature, and multi-colour blackbody spectral luminosity to higher energy bands. Furthermore, we identify a clear structural degeneracy: a static or slowly rotating black hole embedded in a dense MCG structure can elevate radiative efficiencies up to $η\approx 6.5\%$. This framework is presented as a structured proposal to quantify environmental systematic uncertainties in standard black hole spin-estimation techniques.

</details>


### [7] [Magnetic Reconnection and Energy Extraction from a Rotating Black Hole in a Four-dimensional Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04449)
*Abdul Malik Sultan,Muhammad Israr Aslam,Muhammad Nawaz,Rubab Manzoor,Ke Wang,Hamood Ur Rehman,Yakup Yildirim*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转四维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力黑洞中基于磁重联的能量提取机制，发现即使在低自旋参数（0.4和0.22）下能量提取仍然可行，且Gauss-Bonnet耦合参数α可进一步降低自旋阈值，能量提取功率超过Blandford-Znajek机制，且内落（plunging）区域的能量提取功率高于圆轨道情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Comisso和Asenjo提出了基于能层内等离子体磁重联的新型能量提取机制，本文旨在将该机制推广到旋转四维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力黑洞中，分析其能量提取的功率与效率，并探讨Gauss-Bonnet耦合参数对能量提取的影响。

Method: 首先分析该时空的背景性质及其物理量（事件视界、能层边界及能层大小）；然后分析圆轨道上的磁重联，研究能量提取区域、功率与效率；随后分析内落（plunging）区域的能量提取；最后对比两种区域下的能量提取功率。

Abstract: We investigate quintessential inflation in a nonminimally coupled scalar-tensor theory, parameterizing the post-inflationary radiation abundance independently of the reheating mechanism. Because this temperature dictates the duration of kination and the available scalar-field excursion, it directly constrains the present-day dark-energy equation of state. While a single-exponential coupling achieves the required post-inflationary potential drop, the same constant slope does not provide viable late-time acceleration. A double-exponential deformation resolves this tension by decoupling the average slope governing the total potential drop from the asymptotic slope driving cosmic acceleration. Full numerical solutions confirm this picture, yielding a thawing quintessence regime with $w_{\varphi,0}\simeq (-0.90, -0.95)$ for our benchmarks. Our results demonstrate that future dark-energy measurements can directly probe the post-inflationary reheating history of the Universe.

Abstract: Seismic Newtonian noise, arising from fluctuating gravitational forces on detector test masses due to ambient seismic activity, represents a fundamental sensitivity limit for low-frequency gravitational-wave observatories such as the Einstein Telescope. Abstract: We study the simplicial boundary $\partial\mathcal{FS}$ of Culler-Vogtmann Outer space via thickenings defined by graph-theoretic connectivity. We prove that the inclusion $\partial\mathcal{FS}\hookrightarrow C'$ is $(2n-3)$-connected. The proof shows, more precisely, that adding graphs with cut vertices is a homotopy equivalence, while the only non-contractible fibres in the $2$-bond thickening occur over $θ$-graphs. The result gives further evidence that $\partial\mathcal{FS}$ may be $(2n-3)$-spherical, an $\operatorname{Out}(F_n)$-analogue of Rognes's connectivity conjecture for the common basis complex. Abstract: Vector-valued functions on finite simplicial complexes give rise to multiparameter sublevel set filtrations and corresponding persistent homology. We study the associated inverse problem for multiparameter persistent homology (MPH) of $n$-filters on a fixed finite simplicial complex. We endow both the space of filters and the moduli space of essentially finite persistence modules with stratifications, where strata are given by orbits of natural actions of order-isomorphisms of the unit $n$-cube. The MPH map is then shown to be equivariant and strongly stratified. Over each stratum in the image, the MPH map restricts to a trivial fiber bundle whose fiber is a polyhedral complex. We provide an upper bound on the dimension of the fibers in terms of multigraded Betti numbers, recovering as a special case the known one-parameter bound obtained by Leygonie and Tillmann (2022).

Abstract: We study the effective, connective, and very effective filtrations in the $\mathbb{C}$-motivic, $2$-complete, cellular, stable homotopy category. We do so by using the filtered spectrum model for this category due to Gheorghe-Isaksen-Krause-Ricka, and in particular the motivic analogue functor $Γ_\star$. Then we can express the covers making up the respective filtrations of a nice motivic analogue $Γ_\star(X)$ via filtered spectra, and use these to compute the slices. Applying this in the case of $X$ being the sphere spectrum, $\text{MU}$, $\text{ku}$, or an Eilenberg-MacLane spectrum recovers a number of conjectures due to Voevodsky. Applying it to $\text{ko}$ recovers a computation of Ananyevskiy-Röndigs-Østvær. Abstract: We develop a comprehensive theory of dualizable additive categories. We provide several equivalent characterizations, notably identifying them as separated Grothendieck prestable categories satisfying the $\mathrm{AB4}^*$ and $\mathrm{AB6}$ axioms. We establish a connection to almost mathematics by demonstrating that they arise precisely as the categories of connective almost modules over connective $\mathbb{E}_1$-rings.

As a primary application within analytic geometry, we characterize the category $\mathrm{Nuc}(R)_{\geq 0}$ of connective nuclear modules (in the sense of Clausen--Scholze) over an adic $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-ring $R$ via a universal property, identifying it as the additive rigidification of the category of connective complete $R$-modules.
  Finally, we construct the universal finitary stable localizing invariant for dualizable additive categories, the presentable stable category $\mathcal{M}\mathrm{ot}_{\mathrm{pst}}$ of prestable motives, and demonstrate that its unit corepresents nonconnective algebraic $K$-theory. We prove that the motives of small additive categories and those of dualizable additive categories generate the same presentable stable subcategory.

Abstract: We propose a holographic map from the semiclassical Hilbert space of pure general relativity in $\text{AdS}_{3}$ to that of $\text{CFT}_{2}$. We define the bulk Hilbert space by semiclassically quantising the phase space in the basis of a fixed-area network on a Cauchy slice $Σ$. A fixed-area network is a maximal non-intersecting set of geodesics on $Σ$ whose lengths and angular momenta have been fixed. Our holographic map differentiates between `external' geodesics that are homotopic to a connected component of $\partial Σ$, and `internal' geodesics which are not. The lengths and angular momenta of external geodesics become conformal weights of primaries in the Hilbert space of the CFT living on the corresponding component of $\partial Σ$. The fixed-area network determines the wave function, which is given by a network of OPE coefficients of primaries whose weights are determined by the corresponding lengths. For sufficiently semiclassical states, there is an agreement between bulk and boundary inner products. We apply this proposal to various physics questions. The boundary dual of a bulk gauge turns out to be an emergent basis for sufficiently semiclassical states. We also define boundary operators that measure the lengths of geodesics behind the horizon, again in semiclassical states. Finally, we apply our map to closed universes and find a failure of semiclassicality in simple cases, which can be partially alleviated by the addition of a massive probe.

Abstract: We study wavefunctions of quantum gravity in asymptotically AdS$_3$ spacetimes and their relation to $T\bar T$-deformed torus partition functions. In the CFT limit, the kernel effectively localizes at the asymptotic boundary, recovering the standard holographic relation in which the partition function on the torus is determined by the boundary value of the bulk wavefunction. For finite deformation, the kernel shifts the effective holographic screen away from the asymptotic boundary and broadens it into a finite bulk region. A $T\bar{T}$-deformed partition function at fixed coupling corresponds to a finite-width bulk wavepacket centered around a radial or temporal location set by the deformation scale. In Euclidean signature, the construction arises naturally from wavefunctions of Rindler AdS$_3$, while after a double Wick rotation it admits a Lorentzian interpretation in terms of closed AdS$_3$ torus universes without asymptotic boundaries. Finally, we discuss the extension of the present construction to de Sitter torus universes and its implications for de Sitter holography, and comment on possible generalizations to more general spatial topologies and higher-dimensional spacetimes.

Finally, we leverage the gauged description of the sine dilaton gravity model to propose a novel quantization of the near-horizon dynamics of ultracold black holes. This leads to a new proposal for the partition function where states in the asymptotics of the spectrum are suppressed dynamically, and features of a discretized spacetime arise. We obtain a non-perturbative low temperature behavior of the partition function, which is quite different than the cold and Nariai physics. Our result can be interpreted as a dynamical way to enforce cosmic censorship on the quantum system.

### [54] [Iwahori Fundamental Local Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04207)
*Taeuk Nam*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了三个温和分歧（tamely ramified）的因子化模范畴的局部等价：第一个是点上的Arkhipov-Bezrukavnikov等价的因子化版本，第二个是点上的Bezrukavnikov等价的因子化版本，第三个是Iwahori分歧的因子化基本局部等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文的动机是将已知的、在点层面成立的（如Bezrukavnikov等价等）局部几何表示论中的关键等价推广到因子化模范畴（factorization module categories）的框架中，从而在更高的结构和更一般的框架下建立这些等价关系。

Method: 作者构造了三个具体的温和分歧局部等价：1）Arkhipov-Bezrukavnikov等价的因子化版本（在点上），2）Bezrukavnikov等价的因子化版本（在点上），3）Iwahori分歧版本的因子化基本局部等价（Fundamental Local Equivalence）。

Result: 成功构造了三个因子化模范畴之间的温和分歧局部等价，将三个已知的经典等价在因子化框架中实现。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了因子化背景下的三个关键局部等价，为后续在更高结构层面的几何表示论研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We construct three tamely ramified local equivalences of factorization module categories. The first is a factorization version of the Arkhipov-Bezrukavnikov equivalence at a point. The second is a factorization version of the Bezrukavnikov equivalence at a point. The third is an Iwahori-ramified version of the factorizable Fundamental Local Equivalence.

</details>


### [55] [Smooth affine surfaces properly dominated by $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04214)
*Buddhadev Hajra*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文分类了所有允许从 C*×C* 到自身存在有限满射态射的光滑复仿射曲面。利用对数小平维数的分类理论，证明了这样的曲面其对数小平维数只能是 -∞ 或 0，并确定了所有可能性：维数为 -∞ 的只有 C² 和 C×C*，维数为 0 的恰为 C*×C* 和 Fujita 曲面 H[-1,0,-1]。这证实了 M. Furushima 在 1989 年提出的分类猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在完成光滑复仿射曲面被 C*×C* 正确支配（即存在有限满射态射）时的分类问题。这一问题由 M. Furushima 于 1989 年提出预期分类，但长期未得到严格证明。该分类对理解仿射曲面在有限态射下的行为以及对数小平维数理论的应用具有重要意义。

Method: 本文采用光滑仿射曲面的分类理论（依据对数小平维数进行分类），通过分析有限态射在曲面上诱导的代数几何结构性质，结合 C*×C* 自身的特殊几何（如其为平坦群簇等），逐类考察不同对数小平维数情形下曲面所允许的结构，最终给出完整的可能性枚举。

Result: 证明了被 C*×C* 有限支配的光滑仿射曲面的对数小平维数只能为 -∞ 或 0。具体地，对数小平维数为 -∞ 的曲面仅有 C² 和 C×C*；对数小平维数为 0 的曲面仅有 C*×C* 和 Fujita 曲面 H[-1,0,-1]。该结果完整确定了所有满足条件的曲面，验证了 Furushima 的预期分类。

Conclusion: 该工作圆满解决了光滑仿射曲面被 C*×C* 正确支配的分类问题，确认了 1989 年 Furushima 所预期的分类清单。这一结果不仅深化了对仿射曲面广泛类的研究，也展示了对数小平维数理论在有限支配分类问题中的强大威力，为后续相关代数几何研究提供了坚实基石。

Abstract: We classify smooth complex affine surfaces admitting a finite surjective morphism from $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$. Using the classification theory of smooth affine surfaces according to logarithmic Kodaira dimension, we show that every such surface has logarithmic Kodaira dimension either $-\infty$ or $0$, and determine all possibilities. More precisely, the only surfaces of logarithmic Kodaira dimension $-\infty$ are $\mathbf{C}^2$ and $\mathbf{C}\times\mathbf{C}^*$, while those of logarithmic Kodaira dimension $0$ are precisely $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ and Fujita's surface $H[-1,0,-1]$. This establishes the classification of smooth affine surfaces properly dominated by $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ anticipated by M.~Furushima in 1989.

</details>


### [56] [Prym--Tyurin varieties coming from intermediate coverings of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04298)
*Víctor Valdebenito Sepúlveda*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的几何构造方法，从光滑曲线的Zp×Zp-Galois覆盖的中间覆盖出发，构造奇素数指数p的Prym-Tyurin簇。作者精确确定了该构造何时产生Prym-Tyurin簇：当基曲线为椭圆曲线时，以及当各向同性étale覆盖的基曲线为亏格2曲线时。该构造具有显式性，使得可以分析相关Prym-Tyurin簇的几何性质，并证明相应的Prym-Tyurin映射（由生成向量的选择索引）到其像上是一般有限的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prym-Tyurin簇是代数几何中一类重要的阿贝尔簇，但现有的构造方法往往难以显式描述，导致对其几何性质的分析十分困难。本文旨在通过一种新的中间覆盖构造方法，为奇素数指数的Prym-Tyurin簇提供显式的几何构造，从而为深入研究其几何性质奠定基础。

Method: 本文的核心方法是利用Zp×Zp-Galois覆盖的中间覆盖来构造Prym-Tyurin簇。具体而言：考虑光滑曲线上的Zp×Zp-Galois覆盖，取其中间覆盖并分析其诱导的Prym-Tyurin结构。作者精确刻画了该构造有效性的条件（基曲线为椭圆曲线，或亏格2曲线上各向同性étale覆盖的情形），并利用构造的显式性来分析所得Prym-Tyurin簇的几何，进而证明由生成向量索引的Prym-Tyurin映射是一般有限的。

Result: 作者精确确定了该几何构造产生Prym-Tyurin簇的条件：基曲线为椭圆曲线，或亏格2曲线的各向同性étale覆盖情形。在此基础上，证明了所构造的Prym-Tyurin簇具有可分析的几何结构，且相应的Prym-Tyurin映射对像是一般有限的。

Conclusion: 该论文为奇素数指数p的Prym-Tyurin簇提供了一种新的显式几何构造方案，推广了已有构造方法，并为后续研究Prym-Tyurin簇的模空间结构、纤维化及其在代数曲线理论中的应用提供了新工具。

Abstract: We introduce a new geometric construction of Prym--Tyurin varieties of odd prime exponent $p$ arising from intermediate coverings of $\mathbb Z_p\times\mathbb Z_p$--Galois covers of smooth curves. We determine precisely when these coverings give rise to Prym--Tyurin varieties, namely when the base curve is elliptic and in the case of isotropic étale covers over curves of genus $2$. The explicit nature of the construction makes it possible to analyze the geometry of the associated Prym--Tyurin varieties and to prove that the corresponding Prym--Tyurin maps, indexed by the choice of a generating vector, are generically finite onto their images.

</details>


### [57] [On the topology of fibers of complex polynomial maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04320)
*Laurentiu Maxim,John Messina*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文综述了复多项式映射纤维上同调的研究现状，重点关注消失范围，并在分岔值处建立了多项式消失上同调的新结果，同时推导了正常纤维和非典型纤维首个可能非零贝蒂数的上界，将孤立奇点情形下的结果推广到具有任意奇点的多项式映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复多项式映射纤维的同调结构是代数几何和奇点理论中的核心问题。已有的许多结果（如Tibăr、Siersma、Dimca等人的工作）仅限于孤立奇点（包括无穷远奇点）的情形，而实际中多项式映射可能具有任意类型的奇点，因此亟需将这些结果推广到更一般的情形。

Method: 本文首先系统综述了复多项式映射纤维上同调的已知结果，特别是消失范围的相关结论；然后针对分岔值处的消失上同调建立了新定理；最后利用局部奇点不变量推导出正常纤维和非典型纤维首个可能非零贝蒂数的上界估计。

Result: 在多项式映射具有任意奇点（不限于孤立奇点）的广义框架下，建立了分岔值处消失上同调的新结果，并给出了正常纤维和非典型纤维第一个可能非零贝蒂数的上界，推广了Tibăr、Siersma、Dimca等人关于孤立奇点的经典定理。

Conclusion: 该工作将复多项式纤维上同调的研究从孤立奇点情形推进到任意奇点情形，为理解一般多项式映射的拓扑结构提供了更完整的理论框架，拓展了纤维上同调的消失范围及贝蒂数界估计的适用范围。

Abstract: We survey known results on the cohomology of fibers of complex polynomial maps, with particular emphasis on vanishing ranges, and establish new results on the vanishing cohomology of a polynomial at a bifurcation value. We also derive upper bounds for the first possibly nonvanishing Betti number of general and atypical fibers in terms of local singularity invariants. These results extend several theorems of Tibăr, Siersma, Dimca, and others from the case of isolated singularities, including singularities at infinity, to polynomial maps with arbitrary singularities.

</details>

Main category: math.AG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shimada最近证明了Frobenius–Witt余切复形（即Saito的Frobenius–Witt微分形式的动画化）在perfectoid环上消失，因而可作为"绝对"余切复形的候选。本文旨在进一步理解该对象的结构，并将其推广到更广泛的代数范畴。

Method: 作者通过给出Frobenius–Witt余切复形的算术扩张的拉回描述，获得其直接刻画。基于此，将结果推广到导出环和动画预对数环，并利用该描述计算导出δ-环的Frobenius–Witt余切复形。同时提出了相对于导出δ-环的Frobenius–Witt余切复形版本。

Result: 建立了Frobenius–Witt余切复形在导出环和动画预对数环上的推广；成功计算了导出δ-环的Frobenius–Witt余切复形；对于对数（prisms），建立了Frobenius–Witt余切复形的消失定理，推广了perfectoid环上的对应结果。

Conclusion: 本文通过拉回描述给出了一种系统方法，将Frobenius–Witt余切复形推广到衍生与动画设置，并证明了在prisms上的消失性，从而扩展了此前仅在perfectoid环上成立的消失结果，为"绝对"余切复形理论提供了更广泛的框架支撑。

Abstract: Shimada recently showed that Frobenius--Witt cotangent complex, the animation of Saito's Frobenius--Witt differentials, vanishes on perfectoid rings, thus it serves as a candidate of ``absolute'' cotangent complex.
  In this article, we give a pullback description of their arithmetic extension, and as a consequence, we give a direct description of Frobenius--Witt cotangent complex, which leads to generalizations to derived rings and animated pre-log rings. This description allows us to compute Frobenius--Witt cotangent complex of derived $δ$-rings as well.
  We also propose a version of Frobenius--Witt cotangent complex relative to derived $δ$-rings, and establish a vanishing result for prisms, which generalizes vanishing of Frobenius--Witt cotangent complex of perfectoid rings.

</details>

*Narasimha Chary Bonala,Yogendra Singh*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了光滑广义Bott-Samelson簇的Gromov宽度，证明了配备有理Kähler形式的这类簇的Gromov宽度由其次小有理曲线的辛面积给出，并由此获得了这些簇关于丰富线丛的Seshadri常数的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bott-Samelson簇是Schubert簇的经典分解方案，Perrin将其推广为广义Bott-Samelson簇。研究这类投影簇的辛几何性质（特别是Gromov宽度）是理解其作为辛流形结构的核心问题，同时Gromov宽度与代数几何中的Seshadri常数之间存在密切联系，这一研究有助于建立辛几何与代数几何之间的桥梁。

Method: 作者对配备有理Kähler形式的广义Bott-Samelson簇进行了Gromov宽度的计算。通过确定这类簇的极小有理曲线的辛面积，建立了Gromov宽度的精确刻画。利用Gromov宽度与Seshadri常数之间的已知关系（Seshadri常数通常由Gromov宽度给出上界），进一步推导出关于丰富线丛的Seshadri常数的上界估计。

Result: 证明了广义Bott-Samelson簇（配备有理Kähler形式）的Gromov宽度等于其极小有理曲线的辛面积。作为推论，得到了这些簇关于丰富线丛的Seshadri常数的上界。

Conclusion: 本文给出了广义Bott-Samelson簇Gromov宽度的精确公式，并通过这一辛几何结果获得了代数几何中Seshadri常数的新的上界估计，深化了对这类簇的辛拓扑和代数几何性质的理解。

Abstract: We study the Gromov width of smooth generalized Bott-Samelson varieties, a class of projective varieties constructed by Perrin in \cite{Per07} as a generalization of classical Bott-Samelson resolutions of Schubert varieties. We show that the Gromov width of such a variety equipped with a rational Kähler form is given by the symplectic area of its minimal rational curves. As a consequence, we obtain upper bounds for the Seshadri constants of these varieties with respect to ample line bundles.

</details>

Result: (1) 对任意单枝概形X，H^{bA^1}(X) 同构于其各函数域的 H^{bA^1} 之积；(2) 特别地，对簇V，H^{bA^1}(V) ≃ H^{bA^1}(k(V))；(3) 单枝概形的双有理等价可由其通用纤维的双有理可缩性检测；(4) 稳定双有理态射与纯超越域扩张诱导完全忠实嵌入。

Abstract: We promote the $n$-birational motivic homotopy category $S\mapsto \mathcal{H}^n(S)$ to a $Pr^L$-valued presheaf on $Corr(\mathrm{Sch})_{uglt,sm}$. As a consequence, for any unibranch scheme $X$, its (zeroth) birational motivic homotopy category $\mathcal{H}^{b\mathbb{A}^1}(X)$ decomposes as the product of the birational motivic homotopy categories of its function fields; in particular, for a variety $V$, $\mathcal{H}^{b\mathbb{A}^1}(V) \simeq \mathcal{H}^{b\mathbb{A}^1}(k(V))$. This implies that birational equivalences of unibranch schemes in $Sm_S$ can be detected by the birational contractibility of their generic fibers. Finally, we establish that stably birational morphisms and purely transcendental field extensions induce fully faithful embeddings of birational motivic homotopy categories.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 策略上融合三条理论工具：(1) Simpson 对光滑适当 DM 栈建立的非阿贝尔 Hodge 对应；(2) Mochizuki 将经典 Simpson 对应推广到对数（log）情形的结果；(3) Deng-Cadorel 的一致化结果。通过这三者的结合，刻画紧化的结构参数并构造边界除子为 $[A/G]$ 无交并的紧化。

Result: 成功刻画了光滑适当 DM 栈成为球商栈紧化的条件，并证明了每个 $d$ 维球商都存在一种紧化，其边界除子是无交并的商栈 $[A/G]$（$A$ 为阿贝尔簇，$G$ 为有限群），推广了 Deng-Cadorel 的已有结论。

Conclusion: 作者通过结合非阿贝尔 Hodge 对应、对数 Simpson 对应和一致化结果，建立了球商紧化边界除子的一种统一结构描述，为理解球商栈的几何与算术性质提供了新的视角，并推广了 Deng-Cadorel 的先前工作。

Abstract: We characterize smooth proper Deligne-Mumford stacks $\mathscr{X}$ that arise as compactifications of ball quotient stacks $[\mathbb{B}^d/Γ]$. Moreover, we show that every ball quotient admits a compactification whose boundary divisor $\mathscr{D}:=\mathscr{X}-[\mathbb{B}^d/Γ]$ is a disjoint union of quotient stacks $[A/G]$, where $A$ is an abelian variety and $G$ is a finite group. This generalizes a result of Deng-Cadorel. Our strategy combines Simpson's non-abelian Hodge correspondence for smooth proper DM-stacks, Mochizuki's generalization of the classical Simpson's correspondence to the log setting, and the uniformization results of Deng-Cadorel.

### [62] [Joyce's invariant and Virasoro Constraints for Quot schemes on curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04795)

Main category: math.AG

<details>

Abstract: For an arbitrary Artin algebra $A$, we construct a minimal and consistent scattering diagram by approximating its module category $\mathrm{mod}\,A$ using the subcategories $(\mathrm{mod}\,A)_\ell$ of modules of length at most $\ell \in \mathbb{N}$. We prove that each subcategory $(\operatorname{mod}A)_\ell$ possesses a well-behaved lattice of torsion classes, a finite wall-and-chamber structure $\mathfrak{D}_\ell(A)$ and an associated picture group $G_\ell(A)$ with a natural categorical interpretation. Using these properties, we build a finite, minimal, consistent scattering diagram for each $\ell \in \mathbb{N}$. Passing to the inverse limit, we establish the existence of a minimal consistent scattering diagram for $A$. In particular, when $A$ is a finite-dimensional algebra over $\mathbb{C}$, our inverse limit construction is canonically isomorphic to Bridgeland's stability scattering diagram.

The coupling of the $D_{s1}(2700)$ to its constituents, $DK^{*}$, is constrained by the available experimental measurement of $R_{D_{s1}} =Γ(D_{s1}(2700) \to D^{*}K)/Γ(D_{s1}(2700)\to DK)=0.91\pm0.13\pm0.12$. With the resulting coupling, the total decay width can be readily obtained and is found to be in good agreement with the experimental measurement, providing strong support for interpreting the $D_{s1}(2700)$ as a $P$-wave $DK^{*}$ molecular state. Abstract: Moving a detector through a beam with a spatially varying energy spectrum exposes the same target and apparatus to distinct incident spectra, enabling interaction spectroscopy. While movable neutrino detectors were put forth primarily to control systematic uncertainties, we show that detector motion enables probing the structure of fundamental interactions. We demonstrate this with the Intermediate Water Cherenkov Detector (IWCD), the movable detector of Hyper-Kamiokande in the J-PARC off-axis beam, considering neutrino non-standard interactions (NSI) as a benchmark. Exploiting ratios of neutral current to charged current event rates reduces common normalization uncertainties, while measurements at multiple off-axis positions can break degeneracies that persist for a single incident spectrum. Combining three off-axis positions and adopting a $5\%$ correlated normalization uncertainty benchmark, we project $95\%$ CL sensitivities to axial neutral current NSI of $-0.07 \lesssim \varepsilon^{uA}_{μμ} \lesssim 0.06$ and vector NSI of $-0.10 \lesssim \varepsilon^{uV}_{μμ} \lesssim 0.12$, as well as for charged current NSI of $-0.05 \lesssim \varepsilon^{udL}_{μμ} \lesssim 0.05$. Axial NSI, which do not contribute to the ordinary matter potential, are complementary to neutrino oscillation and high energy scattering measurements. More broadly, detector motion provides a new way to distinguish interactions with different energy dependence.

</details>

Abstract: Many quarkoniumlike states have been observed above open-flavor thresholds, but their organization and internal structure remain unsettled. Their binding energies, radii, and small quarkonium components are highly sensitive to the adjoint meson mass, whereas the higher spectrum is more stable. Together with the hybrid reference levels, the spectrum provides multiplet assignments for most candidates. States not naturally accommodated point to the need for hidden-strange and $S+P$ tetraquark/open-flavor BO sectors and for hybrid--tetraquark and hybrid--quarkonium mixings.

Nonlinear fragmentation reduces the comoving axion number and modifies the relation between the misalignment history and present relic abundance. For sufficiently large axion masses, strong self-interactions drive the small-scale spectrum towards the attractor found for post-inflationary axions, so that distinct early histories can lead to similar late-time structures.

Abstract: I discuss the evaluation of the effective potential and related quantities starting from bare perturbation theory followed by $\overline{\rm{MS}}$ renormalization. Abstract: Neutrinoless double beta decay is the flagship laboratory probe of a Majorana contribution to the neutrino mass. Recently, innovative detector technologies as used in the proposed NuDoubt++ experiment are changing the game. In this work, we explore how a positron- and electron-mode detector can be complementary in the search for new physics. Assuming an observation of neutrinoless double beta decay, we predict the expected discovery half-life for the positron-modes. Using half-life ratios, especially between the double beta and electron capture modes, we demonstrate how underlying long-range interactions can be distinguished, in particular to identify a purely right-handed leptonic current.

Abstract: We investigate the in-medium properties of the open-charm vector mesons $D_s^{*}$ and $D^{*}$ in hot and dense nuclear matter within the framework of finite-temperature and finite-density QCD sum rules. The masses exhibit a non-monotonic dependence on baryon density, whereas the leptonic decay constants decrease monotonically throughout the investigated density range. Increasing temperature generally weakens the density-induced modifications, although baryon density remains the dominant driver of the in-medium evolution. The largest mass shifts occur at intermediate-to-high densities, reaching approximately $-413~\mathrm{MeV}$ for the $D_s^{*-}$ meson and $-207~\mathrm{MeV}$ for the $D^{*-}$ meson, while the leptonic decay constants are reduced by more than $68\%$ in both channels at the highest densities considered. We further investigate the particle--antiparticle splittings of the masses and leptonic decay constants induced by finite baryon density. Finite baryon density lifts the vacuum degeneracy between the charge-conjugate states, while increasing temperature generally suppresses the resulting asymmetries. Although the strange and non-strange channels exhibit similar qualitative behavior, quantitative differences emerge in both the in-medium modifications and the particle--antiparticle splittings. ....

Abstract: We investigate light quark energy loss in a quark-gluon plasma using the holographic falling string setup. This geometry mirrors that of recent shooting string simulations, enabling a direct comparison between integrated stopping lengths and differential energy loss. Systematic scans over temperature, baryon chemical potential, and path length reveal that near the QCD crossover, plasmas with more dynamical flavors exhibit stronger quenching, consistent with RHIC strange hadron suppression data. At higher temperatures, approaching the conformal regime, this flavor hierarchy reverses, a trend absent in flavorless models. Finite baryon chemical potential shortens stopping distances and enhances energy loss, with marked sensitivity near the QCD critical endpoint, mirroring anomalies observed in RHIC beam energy scans. Consistent trends from these two independent holographic observables rule out formalism specific artifacts and support the reliability of our lattice calibrated EMD framework. We further discuss the geometric origin of the high temperature flavor ordering, parameter sensitivities, and possible extensions to heavy ion transport simulations.

Abstract: Multiple lattice evidences support the existence of a confining but chirally symmetric stringy fluid regime of QCD above the chiral symmetry restoration temperature at T_{ch} ~ 155 MeV. This regime is characterized by an approximate chiral spin symmetry and its extensions which means that the propagating excitations represent the chirally symmetric quarks connected into color singlets by the chromoelectric string. Clear π,π' peaks above T_{ch} were extracted on the lattice from the spatial and temporal correlators, which become broader with temperature and disappear roughly at $3T_{ch}$. The meson-like excitations above T_{ch} were studied within the manifestly confining and chirally symmetric model. It has been demonstrated that the chiral symmetry restoration in the confining regime happens because of Pauli blocking of the levels, required for the existence of the quark condensate, by the thermal quark excitation. The same Pauli blocking leads to a huge swelling of the low-spin mesons above T_{ch} which become infinitely large in the chiral limit. This property should be crucial for the explanation of the high collectivity and a very small mean-free path of the constituents above $T_{ch}$ seen experimentally. Here we demonstrate that the swelling of pions above T_{ch} is a model-independent effect required by current algebra.

This provides a compact production-side CP measurement for axionlike particles and more general spin-zero resonances.

Abstract: The strong CP problem and the origin of neutrino masses are among the clearest motivations for physics beyond the Standard Model. For each scenario, we determine the axion couplings to photons and neutrinos and examine their phenomenological consequences. A robust prediction of all the frameworks considered is the existence of axion-neutrino couplings. We show that these interactions can significantly affect the axion decays. If the QCD axion makes up the observed dark matter, these couplings also lead to a large monochromatic neutrino flux from axion decays, opening a complementary path to test these theories in future neutrino experiments.

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于一般的非厄米哈密顿量，Arnoldi约化虽然产生上Hessenberg矩阵而非厄米Lanczos理论的三对角形式，但其对角和次对角系数中仍存在一个封闭的Toda扇区。Krylov Gram行列式是二维Toda格子的τ函数，其Flaschka变量恰好等于这些Arnoldi系数。次对角系数平方同时决定Fubini–Study度量和Berry曲率，且Arnoldi框架联络提供精确等谱输运的厄米三对角生成元，可实现逆绝热驱动。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将有限维非厄米哈密顿量与循环态向量按全纯方式变形，分析Arnoldi约化中上Hessenberg矩阵的对角与次对角系数。通过证明Krylov Gram行列式为有限二维Toda格子的τ函数，建立Arnoldi系数与Flaschka变量的精确对应。在此基础上，考察次对角系数与Krylov子空间的Fubini–Study度量和Berry曲率的关系，并在Arnoldi框架下构造联络生成元以实现精确等谱输运和逆绝热驱动。

Result: 发现Toda动力学在Arnoldi约化的对角和次对角系数上闭合，不依赖于其余Hessenberg元。次对角系数平方决定全纯Krylov子空间的几何量，且在Arnoldi崩溃时丢失子空间对应的几何量不再可定义。沿光滑实路径，Arnoldi框架联络生成一个厄米三对角矩阵，可实现精确等谱输运；当矩阵可对角化且谱非简并时，该生成元抵消瞬时本征空间之间的跃迁，实现逆绝热驱动。

Conclusion: 非厄米Arnoldi约化与有限二维Toda可积结构之间存在深刻关联，次对角系数承载了Krylov子空间的几何信息，并可作为逆绝热驱动的生成元。这一框架为Krylov复杂度、量子几何和绝热输运的统一描述提供了新的数学基础。

Abstract: Although Arnoldi reduction of a generally non-Hermitian Hamiltonian yields an upper Hessenberg matrix rather than the tridiagonal form of Hermitian Lanczos theory, we show that a closed Toda sector survives in its diagonal and subdiagonal coefficients. For a fixed finite-dimensional Hamiltonian and a cyclic state vector deformed holomorphically, the Krylov Gram determinants are $τ$ functions of the finite two-dimensional Toda lattice, whose Flaschka variables coincide exactly with these Arnoldi coefficients. The Toda dynamics therefore closes on this sector without determining the remaining upper Hessenberg entries. The subdiagonal part of the same sector also has a direct geometric meaning: the squared subdiagonal coefficients determine both the Fubini--Study metric and the Berry curvature of holomorphic Krylov subspaces, whereas the geometric quantities associated with subspaces lost at Arnoldi breakdown cease to be defined. Along a smooth real path in the cyclic region, the Arnoldi-frame connection further provides a Hermitian tridiagonal generator of exact isospectral transport. When added to the Arnoldi matrix, this generator cancels transitions between instantaneous eigenspaces and realizes counterdiabatic driving whenever the matrix is diagonalizable with a nondegenerate spectrum.

</details>

Conclusion: 量子相对熵最小变化原理为量子统计推断提供了统一框架，softmin热测量类比于统计力学中的热态，为测量理论建立了新的结构联系，扩展了量子Bayes规则的理论基础。

Abstract: The minimum change principle provides an information-theoretic characterization of the Bayes reversal channel in classical probability theory and has recently been proposed as a framework for extending Bayes' rule to quantum information theory. Using quantum relative entropy, we investigate a minimum change principle for the setting of quantum statistical inference. Specifically, we consider a forward process based on a classical-to-quantum preparation channel and a reverse process based on a quantum-to-classical measurement channel. We establish a closed-form characterization of measurements that are optimal for this principle, and this optimal measurement can be found via a dual formulation involving a single unconstrained Hermitian variable. This perspective allows us to recover some notable measurements within the same framework, including pretty good measurements and Fermi-Dirac thermal measurements, and we use it to discover a novel family that we call softmin thermal measurements. We further show that softmin thermal measurements arise as optimal solutions to entropy-regularized semidefinite optimization problems, demonstrating that they play a role for measurements analogous to that of thermal states in statistical mechanics. Finally, we prove an additivity property for the relative-entropy minimum change principle and investigate the performance of Fermi-Dirac thermal measurements for quantum hypothesis testing.

</details>

Abstract: We study the ultimate quantum limit of rotation sensing with a few strongly interacting bosons confined in a quasi-one-dimensional ring trap with two weak links. It is demonstrated that a self-consistent many-body solution of the problem is required to correctly predict the ultimate sensitivity of this strongly correlated many-body gyroscope to rotation. For both small rotation velocities and small particle numbers, the many-body quantum Fisher information becomes maximal for large interaction couplings, showing the potential of strongly interacting miniaturized many-body sensors to precisely estimate slow rotations with high spatial resolution.

</details>


### [96] [Graph-Aware Exact Branch-and-Bound with Device Profiles for Static Qubit Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04058)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态量子比特分配将逻辑量子比特映射到稀疏物理设备上，需最小化交互加权物理距离代价，本质上是矩形二次分配问题（QAP）。现有方法虽结合了强下界与分布式分支定界，但仍有大量计算资源消耗，需要更轻量且高效的精确求解路径。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级"分配-边界"路径，包含四个工程化组件：(1) 不可避免分配代价过滤（unavoidable assigned-cost filtering）；(2) 增量维护的根轨道与前缀稳定器对称性剪枝（root-orbit和prefix-stabilizer symmetry pruning）；(3) 条件化父级LAP（线性分配问题）筛选；(4) 电路无关的物理设备配置文件（device profiles）。这些组件与整数线性规划归约（COP/GLB下界）相结合，支撑分支定界搜索。

Result: 在21个相对容易的Melbourne实例和6个GLB基线完成的Boeblingen实例上，最终单线程配置分别获得2.98倍和13.27倍几何平均加速比。在60线程共享内存服务器上，Boeblingen-Cairo实验的所有实例均在半小时内得到最优性证明（不含一次性设备工件构建时间）。

Conclusion: 图感知的节点处理与工程化的搜索过程优化能显著降低精确量子比特分配所需的计算资源，验证了在强下界之外通过系统性剪枝和成本过滤来提升求解效率的有效性。

Abstract: Static qubit allocation maps a circuit's logical qubits to a sparse physical device while minimising an interaction-weighted physical-distance cost function, yielding a rectangular quadratic assignment problem. Existing work combines strong lower bounds with distributed branch-and-bound. We integrate graph-aware exact reductions with an engineering bundle for a lightweight assignment-bound path: unavoidable assigned-cost filtering, incrementally maintained root-orbit and prefix-stabilizer symmetry pruning, conditioned parent-LAP screening, and circuit-independent physical device profiles. On 21 relatively easy Melbourne instances and six Boeblingen instances completed by the GLB baseline, the final single-thread configuration provides geometric-mean speedups of 2.98x and 13.27x, respectively. With 60 threads on one shared-memory server, all instances in the final Boeblingen--Cairo experiment are certified optimal within half an hour, excluding one-time device-artifact construction. These results show that graph-aware node processing and engineering the search process substantially reduce the resources required for exact allocation.

</details>


### [97] [Reconstructing non-Abelian braiding and fusion without anyon transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04103)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 编织和融合变换以平均归一化输出态保真度F_R=0.9988和F_F=0.9987再现了其理想作用。组合这些原语产生了非Clifford编织和非稳定子资源态，支持测量-only任意子编码作为更大拓扑编码量子处理器的构建模块。

Conclusion: 测量-only协议成功在现有量子硬件上高效重构了非阿贝尔任意子的编织与融合基本操作，高保真度验证了该方法的可行性，为构建更大规模拓扑编码量子处理器奠定了基础。

Abstract: Non-Abelian anyons offer a route to fault-tolerant and universal quantum computing, but experimental access to their defining braiding and fusion data remains limited by the resource overhead of implementing extended anyonic processes on quantum hardware. Here we introduce and experimentally realise a measurement-only protocol based on temporally ordered ribbon operations that reconstructs the non-Abelian braiding and fusion primitives of the quantum double model $D(S_3)$ without physical anyon transport. We implement a reduced two-qutrit version of the protocol on Quantinuum's H2 trapped-ion processors, realising ancilla-assisted ribbon operations and anyonic charge projections in a qubit encoding. We reconstruct the squared braiding phases and fusion amplitudes using an adapted Hadamard test and post-selected measurements, respectively. The associated braiding and fusion transformations reproduce their ideal actions with average normalised output-state fidelities of $\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{R}=0.9988$ and $\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{F}=0.9987$. Combining these primitives produces a non-Clifford braid and a non-stabilizer resource state, supporting measurement-only anyonic encodings as building blocks for larger topologically encoded quantum processors.

</details>

Abstract: The scale of Neutral Atom (NA) quantum computers requires automated compilation tools. Designing the required heuristic methods demands a deep understanding of complex hardware trade-offs, for which visualizations can provide crucial insights. This work introduces NAViz, the first publicly available app to visualize quantum computations on NA devices in real-time. A case study demonstrates how NAViz was instrumental in identifying and resolving inefficiencies in an existing compilation strategy, leading to a new, more performant one. The tool is available as part of the Munich Quantum Toolkit (MQT) at https://github.com/munich-quantum-toolkit/naviz.

</details>

Abstract: We establish scaling relations governing the equilibrium thermodynamics of real-spectrum non-Hermitian Dirac fermions in a magnetic field. Assuming thermalization with respect to the quasi-Hermitian Hamiltonian, a similarity transformation maps the system at the same applied field onto a Hermitian Dirac model with reduced velocity, while the Landau-level spectrum also admits a representation in terms of a reduced effective magnetic field. This structure yields scaling relations for the chemical potential, entropy, heat capacities, and orbital magnetic response in different thermodynamic ensembles. At fixed projected filling factor (PFF), the self-consistent chemical potential follows the compressed ladder of Landau levels, and the canonical thermodynamic functions are rescaled Hermitian responses. At fixed chemical potential, Landau-level crossings generate oscillatory caloric and magnetic responses governed by the same spectral compression. Quasistatic non-Hermitian deformation at fixed PFF further yields adiabatic temperature scaling. More broadly, these results establish a thermodynamic framework for real-spectrum non-Hermitian quantum matter and provide a starting point for incorporating the effects of interactions and disorder within the same formalism.

Motivation: 量子处理器中的本征噪声过程往往难以隔离，因为多种错误机制对同一相干损失测量均有贡献。为了识别和表征特定类型的控制噪声（即由轴向运动诱导的噪声），作者需要一种能够辨别不同噪声来源的方法。

Method: 采用抗退相干的量子噪声谱学方法，对可单独寻址的离子阱处理器中的控制噪声进行重构。通过将离子在光束轮廓内定位变化，分离曲率依赖的轴向运动噪声与曲率无关的本征控制噪声，并提取运动学参数。同时将该协议并行应用于四离子寄存器。

Result: 证明了热轴向运动噪声主要由局部光束曲率主导，表现为重构控制噪声谱中的低频贡献；通过改变离子位置成功分离了轴向运动噪声与本征控制噪声，并在四离子寄存器上实现了位置相关控制噪声的同步表征。

Conclusion: 研究确定了光束拐点（inflection points）作为抑制轴向运动诱导噪声的工作区域，但代价是降低Rabi速率，该结论与PRX Quantum 3, 010334 (2022)的发现一致。

Abstract: Native noise processes in quantum processors are often difficult to isolate because multiple error mechanisms contribute to the same measured loss of coherence. Here we use dephasing-robust quantum noise spectroscopy to identify and characterize control noise induced by axial motion in an individually-addressed trapped-ion processor. When the ion motion is transverse to the addressing beam, thermal axial motion couples to the beam profile and produces effective amplitude control noise. We show that this noise is governed primarily by the local beam curvature and appears as a low-frequency contribution to the reconstructed control-noise spectrum. By varying the ion position within the beam profile, we separate curvature-dependent axial-motion noise from curvature-independent native control noise and extract motional parameters that are otherwise difficult to access on this platform. We also apply the protocol in parallel to a four-ion register, demonstrating a spectroscopic method for simultaneous characterization of position-dependent control noise across multiple qubits. The results of this study identify beam inflection points as operating regions that suppress axial-motion-induced noise at the cost of reduced Rabi rate, as found in PRX Quantum 3, 010334 (2022).

</details>


### [101] [The Born Representation Theorem and the Unistochastic Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04354)
*Jacob A. Barandes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个新的关于随机矩阵的构造性定理。第一个是"Born表示定理"，证明任意随机矩阵的每个元素都可以表示为POVM矩阵与PVM矩阵的乘积之迹，即广义的量子Born规则形式。推论表明，若POVM退化为PVM，则该随机矩阵是幺正随机的（unistochastic）。第二个是"Unistochastic定理"，证明通过对底层向量空间进行有界维度的扩张，任意随机矩阵的每个元素都可由两个PVM矩阵的乘积之迹表示，从而可通过边缘化从更大的幺正随机矩阵导出，确立了幺正随机矩阵相对于随机矩阵的某种优先地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子理论与经典概率论之间的深层联系，特别是随机矩阵（经典马尔可夫过程的核心对象）与量子Born规则之间的结构性关联，试图揭示幺正随机矩阵在数学结构上对一般随机矩阵的"民主性"或"支配性"。

Method: 采用自包含的构造性证明方法。第一个定理通过构造POVM和PVM的具体形式，使随机矩阵的每个元素都能写成迹形式；第二个定理通过对底层希尔伯特空间进行有界维度的扩充（dilation），构造两个PVM使得其迹乘积能通过边缘化得到任意随机矩阵的元素。

Result: 证明了两个新定理：Born表示定理（任意随机矩阵元素可写成POVM×PVM的迹）及其推论（POVM为PVM时矩阵为幺正随机的）；Unistochastic定理（通过有界维数扩张，任意随机矩阵可由更大的幺正随机矩阵边缘化得到）。

Conclusion: 论文确立了幺正随机矩阵对一般随机矩阵的构造性优先地位，暗示量子幺正时间演化与经典马尔可夫过程之间存在深刻的数学联系，并讨论了其在离散时间确定性过程和马尔可夫链中的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents self-contained, constructive proofs of two new theorems about stochastic matrices, with direct relevance to quantum theory. The first theorem, herein called the Born Representation Theorem, shows that each entry of any stochastic matrix can be expressed as the trace of a pairwise product of matrices, where the first factor in the pairwise product belongs to a positive-operator-valued measure (POVM) and the second factor belongs to a projection-valued measure (PVM). As its name suggests, this theorem entails that the entries of any stochastic matrix can be expressed in terms of a generalized version of the quantum-theoretic Born rule. It follows as a corollary that if the POVM in this first theorem is a PVM, then the stochastic matrix is unistochastic, meaning that its entries are each the modulus square of the corresponding entry of a unitary matrix of the same size. The second theorem proved in this paper, called the Unistochastic Theorem, then shows that by dilating the underlying vector space by a bounded number of additional dimensions if necessary, each entry of any stochastic matrix can be expressed in terms of the trace of a pairwise product for which both factors belong to PVMs, and can thus be derived via marginalization from a larger unistochastic matrix. This second theorem therefore establishes a kind of primacy of unistochastic matrices over stochastic matrices, and hints at a close connection with unitary time evolution in quantum theory. The paper concludes with a brief discussion of potential applications to discrete-time deterministic processes and Markov chains.

</details>


### [102] [An optical-fibre-integrated buffer for packet-switched quantum networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04093)
*Daniel Spegel-Lexne,Joakim Argillander,Martin Clason,Åsa Claesson,Kenny Hey Tow,Gustavo Lima,João M. B. Pereira,Guilherme B. Xavier*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文演示了一种全光纤集成的量子网络缓冲器，基于再循环回路和光纤存储线，通过超低损耗极化光纤相位调制器实现快速、偏振不敏感的切换，可在缓冲区内实时读取包头并决定量子比特载荷的存储时间。实验实现了最长47微秒的偏振编码量子比特存储与检索，平均量子误码率为1.8%，并可在数小时内稳定运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分组交换量子网络需要在读取路由信息的同时缓冲量子比特载荷，但先前的方案未能在与电信基础设施兼容的全光纤集成架构中提供这一功能。

Method: 采用再循环回路与光纤存储线相结合的缓冲结构，核心组件为超低损耗极化光纤相位调制器，可在光纤内直接实现快速、偏振不敏感的切换，使得包头读取与载荷处理能在缓冲器内部完成。

Result: 实现了偏振编码量子比特载荷最长47微秒的存储与检索，平均量子误码率为1.8%，且系统可在数小时内稳定运行。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于光纤器件的分组级量子网络缓冲架构，可轻松集成到现有电信基础设施中，为量子互联网的部署开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Packet-switched quantum networks require buffers that can delay qubit payloads while routing information is read out in real time. Previous approaches have not provided this functionality in a fully fibre-integrated architecture compatible with telecom infrastructure. Here we demonstrate an optical-fibre-integrated buffer, based on a recirculating loop and a fibre storage line, in which the storage time of a polarisation-encoded qubit payload is determined by readout of an attached packet header. The key component behind this achievement is an ultra-low-loss poled fibre phase modulator, which provides fast, polarisation-insensitive switching directly in fibre and allows header and payload to be processed within the buffer. We demonstrate storage and retrieval of polarisation-encoded qubit payloads for storage times up to 47 $μ$s, with an average quantum bit error rate of 1.8% together with stable operation over several hours. These results establish a practical fibre-based architecture for packet-level quantum network buffering that can easily integrate into the current telecommunication infrastructure opening up new paths for deployment of the quantum internet.

</details>


### [103] [Single-eigenstate test of eigenstate thermalization hypothesis via perturbed eigenstate quench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04696)
*Zhouhao Guo,Jiaju Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并数值验证了一种基于微扰本征态猝灭协议的单本征态特征量，用于诊断本征态热化假说（ETH）。通过向能量本征态施加弱随机扰动打破其平稳性，刻画子系统演化速度随子系统/总系统尺寸比的时均变化，依据S形（ETH满足）与凸J形（ETH违反）曲线的定性区分来判断热化行为。该方法在混沌、可积、多体局域化及量子多体疤痕等一维自旋链基准模型中均与既有热化现象一致，且无需显式构造热系综，为单本征态层面的热化探测提供了鲁棒且实验可行的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETH验证通常依赖系综平均或系统尺寸外推，难以在单个本征态层面直接判断其是否满足热化，且需要显式构建热系综。作者希望提出一种不依赖热系综构造、可在单本征态水平有效且实验可实现的诊断方法。

Method: 提出微扰本征态猝灭协议：向能量本征态引入弱随机扰动以打破其平稳性，随后追踪子系统演化，并以时均子系统演化速度作为子系统尺寸占比的函数进行表征。利用ETH满足态呈现带拐点的S形曲线、ETH违反态呈现凸J形曲线的定性判据进行区分。在可积、混沌、多体局域化及量子多体疤痕等一维自旋链基准体系中进行数值验证。

Result: 在混沌、可积、多体局域化和量子多体疤痕等多类一维自旋链模型中，该判据的判定结果与已确立的热化现象学完全一致，即ETH满足态为S形曲线（拐点在半系统尺寸附近），ETH违反态为凸J形曲线。

Conclusion: 该单本征态诊断方法无需构造显式热系综，提供了一种鲁棒且实验上可行的ETH探针，可在单本征态层面刻画量子系统热化行为，为ETH研究和相关实验验证提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We propose and numerically validate an efficient single-eigenstate diagnostic for the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) based on a perturbed eigenstate quench protocol. By introducing a weak random perturbation to an energy eigenstate to break its stationarity, we characterize the time-averaged subsystem evolution speed as a function of the subsystem-to-total system size ratio. The diagnostic relies on a robust qualitative distinction: eigenstates satisfying ETH exhibit an S-shaped curve with a clear inflection point near half the system size, while ETH-violating eigenstates display a convex J-shaped profile. We benchmark the criterion across paradigmatic one-dimensional spin chains covering chaotic, integrable, many-body localized, and quantum many-body scar regimes, obtaining full agreement with established thermalization phenomenology. Our method circumvents the need for explicit thermal ensemble construction, providing a robust, experimentally feasible probe of eigenstate thermalization at the single-eigenstate level.

</details>


### [104] [Contextuality in Sequential State Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04099)
*Nyan Raess,Farid Shahandeh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了广义语境性在量子态判别协议（包括无歧义判别和最小误差判别）及其序贯版本中的作用。在非序贯情形（n=1）下，通过COPE形式证明不仅最优测量需要语境性，某些非最优测量也需要；在序贯情形（n>1）下，语境性的存在取决于制备的量子态以及所选协议类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态判别是量子信息处理中的基本任务，已知广义语境性在最优量子态判别策略中是必需的。然而，对于序贯判别任务（多个玩家依次尝试判别量子比特的状态）中语境性所扮演的角色尚不清楚。本文旨在系统地研究在不同判别协议（无歧义判别与最小误差判别）及其序贯版本中语境性的作用范围，特别是探索语境性是否不仅在最优测量中出现，也在非最优测量中出现，以及序贯情形下语境性与协议和量子态选择的关系。

Method: 作者采用COPE（通过状态驱动实验的广义语境性）形式主义来分析量子态判别中的语境性。具体地，对于n=1的非序贯情形，研究最优测量和一组特定的非最优测量是否蕴含语境性；对于n>1的序贯情形，分别分析无歧义判别和最小误差判别两种协议，考察语境性的存在如何依赖于制备的量子态和所选择的协议。

Result: 在非序贯（n=1）情形中，通过COPE形式证明语境性的存在不仅限于最优测量，也出现在一组特定的非最优测量中。在序贯（n>1）情形中，发现语境性的存在并非普遍成立，而是依赖于两个关键因素：所制备的量子态的类型，以及所选择的协议（无歧义判别或最小误差判别）。

Conclusion: 广义语境性在量子态判别中的作用比此前认识的更为微妙：虽然它在标准非序贯情形下对最优策略是必需的，但在序贯判别协议中，语境性的出现取决于具体量子态和协议类型的选择，这意味着语境性并非所有判别任务的普适资源，需要在具体情境下确定其必要性。

Abstract: Generalized contextuality is known to be required in optimal strategies for quantum state discrimination protocols. More recently, sequential discrimination tasks have been studied; n players attempt to determine in which state a qubit was prepared, in such a way that they all have a finite probability of success. We consider the extent to which contextuality plays a role in sequential versions of both unambiguous and minimum error discrimination. In the standard nonsequential case where n = 1, we use the COPE formalism to demonstrate that the presence of contextuality is guaranteed not only for the optimal measurement, but for a specific set of nonoptimal measurements as well. In the sequential case n > 1, we show that the presence of contextuality depends on which states are prepared, and on the protocol (unambiguous or minimum error) selected.

</details>


### [105] [Constructive realization of self-referential prediction limits in quantum control: Resource bounds and Gödel-safe architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04779)
*Salman Sajad Wani,Álvaro Perales-Eceiza,Saif Al-Kuwari,Mir Faizal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将从可计算性理论中关于普遍自预测不可能性的形式化结论（Wolpert定理）转化为可在实验室中以有限资源实现的具体物理阻障。利用Kleene递归定理构造对角化协议，任何确定性有界时间预测器都能被嵌入到其自身分析的可逆实验中，并产生与预测相矛盾的经典指针读数。该构造对高效预测器具有多项式编译开销，可具体实现为容错量子电路或最小Mach-Zehnder干涉仪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可编程量子控制系统日益依赖预测模块进行认证、实时反馈和自主决策，一个根本性问题浮现：自分析量子平台能否普遍预测自身的实验结果？Wolpert已形式化证明了普遍自预测的不可能性，但该限制仅停留在数学抽象层面，尚未转化为可在实验室中验证的具体物理阻障，也缺乏对现实量子控制架构的工程设计指导。

Method: 本文采用对角化构造方法，运用Kleene递归定理将任何确定性有界时间预测器转化为一个可逆协议，该协议编码了预测器自身的规格说明，并在运行中调用预测器来预测该规格，最终确定性产生与预测相矛盾的经典指针记录。对于高效预测器，编译开销为多项式级，并给出两种具体物理实现：容错量子电路和最小Mach-Zehnder干涉仪。此外，作者正式定义Gödel安全架构，通过阻断从协议描述到影响指针执行器的因果路径来规避该不可能性。

Result: 本文成功将可计算性理论中的自指悖论转化为可编程量子硬件的具体物理实现：构造了对任何确定性有界时间预测器都成立的矛盾协议，实现了多项式开销的编译，并提供了容错量子电路与Mach-Zehnder干涉仪两种实验方案。同时引入了Gödel安全架构的概念并分析了其在实时量子纠错中的应用，揭示了计算表达力与自我引用限制之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 自参考的极限不再是纯粹的数学抽象，而是自主量子技术可靠运行中必须考虑的明确工程约束。随着量子控制环路的计算表达力不断增强，Gödel安全架构等设计原则将成为构建可验证、可认证的自主量子系统的关键工具。

Abstract: Programmable quantum control systems increasingly rely on predictive modules for certification, real-time feedback, and autonomous decision-making. This development raises a fundamental question: can self-analyzing quantum platforms universally predict their own experimental outcomes? Wolpert formalized a general impossibility of universal self-prediction. Here we translate that limitation into an explicit laboratory obstruction that can be realized with finite resources. We consider settings with programmable quantum control in which predictors can be embedded as subroutines within the experiments they analyze. Our diagonal construction uses Kleene's recursion theorem to transform any deterministic bounded-time predictor into a reversible protocol encoding its own specification. The resulting protocol invokes the predictor on that specification and deterministically produces a classical pointer record that contradicts the forecast. For efficient predictors, the compilation has polynomial overhead and admits concrete physical realizations as a fault-tolerant quantum circuit and as a minimal Mach-Zehnder interferometer. These realizations connect computability-theoretic self-reference to programmable quantum hardware. We also introduce and formally define Gödel-safe architectures. These architectures block the forbidden causal path from the protocol description to an actuator that can affect the pointer during the same run. We analyze their implications for real-time quantum error correction, including the resulting expressiveness trade-offs. As quantum control loops grow in computational expressiveness, the limits of self-reference cease to be mere mathematical abstractions and become explicit engineering constraints for the reliable operation of autonomous quantum technologies.

</details>


### [106] [Constructing Non-Hermitian Theories with Tunable Exceptional Points and Controlled State Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05052)
*Soumya Kanti Pal,Rupak Majumder,Shamik Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出动量空间形变作为构筑非厄米多体哈密顿量中的例外点(EP)的统一设计原理,建立了EP存在的普适判据、临界形变强度以及多体本征矢凝聚的指数增殖规律,并揭示EP可作为任意混合量子态纯化的普适机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 例外点是非厄米量子系统的重要标志,但在多体哈密顿量中如何系统地创造和控制EP仍缺乏一般性设计原则。作者旨在建立一种普适框架,将动量空间形变作为统一的EP工程路线。

Method: 通过动量空间形变(deformation)来构造二次型多体哈密顿量,推导EP存在的普适判据与临界形变强度;分析单动量扇区EP引发的多体本征矢凝聚的指数增殖;建立EP用于混合量子态纯化的机制,揭示纯化区间及体系大小奇偶二分性;提供逆向工程协议以生成短程/长程、互易/非互易非厄米量子物质,并给出显式Lindblad嵌入。

Result: 发现了动量扇区容纳EP的普适判据及对应临界形变强度;单动量扇区EP可导致多体本征矢凝聚的指数增殖;EP被确立为任意混合量子态纯化的普适机制,存在不同纯化区间及热力学极限下的奇偶体系大小二分类;实现了系统性的逆向工程协议以设计多种非厄米量子物质并完成Lindblad嵌入。

Conclusion: 动量空间形变将例外点工程与非厄米多体量子系统的可控设计统一起来,为EP的物理实现与应用提供了完整而系统的理论框架。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EP's) are a hallmark of non-Hermitian quantum systems. We show that momentum-space deformation provides a general design principle for creating and controlling EP's in quadratic many-body Hamiltonians. We identify universal criteria for the momentum sectors to host EP's and the corresponding critical deformation strengths, while revealing that a single momentum-sector EP induces quite remarkably an exponential proliferation of many-body eigenvector coalescences. We further establish EP's as a universal mechanism for purifying arbitrary mixed quantum states, uncovering distinct purification regimes and a fundamental odd-even system-size dichotomy in the thermodynamic limit. Our framework also provides a systematic reverse-engineering protocol for generating short- and long-range, reciprocal and nonreciprocal non-Hermitian quantum matter, together with an explicit Lindblad embedding. These results thus establish momentum-space deformation as a unified route to exceptional-point engineering and controlled design of many-body non-Hermitian quantum systems.

</details>


### [107] [Poled-fibre phase modulator for efficient high-dimensional quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04112)
*Nayda Guerrero,Nelson Villalba,Gustavo H. dos Santos,Carlos J. Salazar,Cristobal Melo,Fernando Castillo,Jaime Cariñe,Guilherme B. Xavier,Esteban S. Gómez,Stephen P. Walborn,João Pereira,Gabriel Saavedra,Gustavo Lima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将极化的光纤相位调制器（PFPM）用于全光纤集成平台中的主动量子态测量，实现四维量子密钥分发，获得了据我们所知超越此前所有报道的有限密钥率，确立了极化光纤电光调制作为高效益检测的通用平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维编码可提升设备无关量子信息协议的噪声鲁棒性和信息容量，但实际实现面临瓶颈：传统基选择需要主动调制器，带来显著光学损耗和偏振敏感问题。需要一种低损耗、偏振无关且与标准通信光纤兼容的技术方案。

Method: 利用极化光纤相位调制器（PFPM）这一光纤原生电光技术，实现单个PFPM完成四维量子比特的基选择，简化接收端架构。在完全光纤集成平台上进行主动量子态测量，并以四维量子密钥分发作为基准实验验证。

Result: 成功在单PFPM架构下实现四维量子态测量，完成四维QKD会话，获得的每脉冲有限秘密密钥率超越了所有此前报道的QKD演示。

Conclusion: 极化光纤电光调制是一种广泛适用的平台，可为光纤集成的量子信息处理提供高效益、低损耗、偏振无关的量子态检测方案，为高维量子协议的实际部署铺平道路。

Abstract: Efficient detection of quantum states underpins advanced device-independent quantum-information protocols that provide the ultimate level of security for tasks including quantum random number generation and quantum key distribution (QKD). High-dimensional encoding is a natural route to boost the performance of such protocols, offering enhanced noise resilience and higher information capacity, yet their practical implementation remains challenging. A key experimental bottleneck in higher dimensions is the typical need of active modulators for basis selection, which incur substantial optical losses and polarization-sensitive operation. Poled optical fiber phase modulators (PFPMs) are a fiber-native electro-optic technology that naturally addresses these challenges, combining sub-dB insertion loss, intrinsic polarization independence, and direct compatibility with standard telecommunications fiber. Here we report the first use of a PFPM for active quantum-state measurements in a fully fiber-integrated platform. Basis selection in our receiver for four-dimensional qudits is achieved using a single PFPM, substantially simplifying the receiver architecture. As a benchmark, we perform a four-dimensional QKD session and obtain a finite secret-key rate per pulse that, to the best of our knowledge, surpasses all previously reported QKD demonstrations. Our results establish poled-fiber electro-optic modulation as a broadly applicable platform for high-efficiency detection in fiber-integrated quantum information processing.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Contextuality and Entanglement-Free Grover Search in a Trapped-Ion Optical Qudit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04128)
*Tarun Dutta,Jasper Phua Sing Cheng,Alex Jin,Sergi Ramos-Calderer,José Ignacio Latorre,Manas Mukherjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在单个 trapped 138Ba+ 离子上实现了可编程的四维光学量子比特（qudit），并演示了无纠缠的 Grover 量子搜索算法（目标态识别概率高达 94.5±2.0%）以及基于 CHSH 型非上下文不等式的量子上下文性验证（最大违犯 S=2.816±0.082，接近 Tsirelson 界）。该工作在一个多能级离子阱平台上同时整合了可编程量子计算与上下文性测量，为研究相干干涉与上下文性之间的关系提供了通用架构，并为高维量子技术的发展提供了可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算优势通常归因于相干干涉等非经典资源，但在多体纠缠天然存在的实验平台上，这些资源的各自作用很难区分。高维量子系统为研究这些资源提供了有吸引力的途径，同时可降低量子信息处理的硬件开销，因此作者希望探索在高维量子系统中区分和利用这些非经典资源。

Method: 作者在单个 138Ba+ 离子中编码可编程的四维光学 qudit，通过相位可编程的光学旋转实现通用的相干控制。利用该平台实现无纠缠的 Grover 搜索算法，并通过 CHSH 型非上下文性不等式测量状态依赖的量子上下文性。

Result: Grover 搜索算法实现目标态识别概率最高达 94.5±2.0%；CHSH 型非上下文性不等式获得最大违犯 S=2.816±0.082，与 Tsirelson 界高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了集可编程量子计算与上下文性测量于一体的多能级离子阱通用架构，为研究相干干涉与上下文性在量子信息处理中的关系提供了新平台，并为高维量子技术提供了可扩展路线。

Abstract: Quantum computational advantage is generally attributed to coherent interference and other non-classical resources, yet their respective roles remain difficult to disentangle in experimental platforms where multipartite entanglement is inherently present. High-dimensional quantum systems provide an attractive route for investigating these resources while simultaneously reducing hardware overhead for quantum information processing. Here we realize a programmable four-dimensional optical qudit encoded in a single trapped $^{138}\mathrm{Ba}^{+}$ ion and demonstrate universal coherent control through phase-programmable optical rotations. Using this platform, we implement an entanglement-free realization of Grover's quantum search algorithm, achieving target-state identification probabilities of up to $94.5\pm2.0\%$. Within the same processor, we further demonstrate state-dependent quantum contextuality through a Clauser--Horne--Shimony--Holt (CHSH)-type noncontextuality inequality, obtaining a maximum violation of $S = 2.816 \pm 0.082$, in close agreement with the Tsirelson bound. By integrating programmable quantum computation and contextuality measurements within a single multilevel trapped-ion platform, our work establishes a versatile architecture for investigating the relationship between coherent interference and contextuality in quantum information processing and provides a scalable route toward high-dimensional quantum technologies.

</details>


### [109] [Sharp Continuity of Petz and Sandwiched Rényi Conditional Entropies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04947)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Po-Chieh Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了优化Petz与sandwiched Rényi条件熵在α∈[1/2,1)阶下关于迹距离的尖锐模连续性界，给出了精确的常数表达式，并证明该界可由各向同性态对达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子条件熵及其Rényi推广是量子信息论的核心量，此前对任意α阶Rényi条件熵的尖锐连续性界尚未确定，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在由各向同性等式族指定的比较点对相关凹Rényi泛函进行线性化；利用Schmidt秩支配将等式几何推广到任意锚定态；结合迹距离对偶性与非交换微扰（calibration）估计控制扰动项和锚定项，且不削弱尖锐常数。证明中的矩阵分析部分借助了AI辅助。

Result: 对于迹距离为δ的两个二分态，两类Rényi条件熵之差至多为(1/(1-α))log[(1-ε)^α+(D-1)^{1-α}ε^α]，其中ε=min{δ,1-1/D}，D为有效维度。对任意δ∈[0,1]，该界可由具有最大纠缠锚的各向同性态对达到。当α→1时恢复Berta等人的量子条件熵尖锐连续性界。

Conclusion: 本文给出了优化Petz与sandwiched Rényi条件熵在α∈[1/2,1)的尖锐模连续性界，该界在α→1极限下一致地还原经典量子条件熵结果，证明显示了线性化与Schmidt秩支配技术的有效性，并揭示了尖锐等式的几何结构。

Abstract: We determine the sharp modulus of continuity, in trace distance, of the optimized Petz and sandwiched Rényi conditional entropies for every order $α\in[\frac12,1)$. If two bipartite states are within trace distance $δ$, then both conditional entropies differ by at most $\frac{1}{1-α}
  \log[(1-\varepsilon)^α
  +(D-1)^{1-α}\varepsilon^α]$, where $\varepsilon := \min\{δ,1-1/D\}$ and $D$ is the effective dimension, given by the dimension of the first subsystem times the largest possible Schmidt rank. For every distance constraint $δ\in[0,1]$, the bound is attained by an isotropic pair with a maximally entangled anchor. Taking $α\uparrow1$ recovers the recent sharp continuity bound of quantum conditional entropy by Berta et al. [arXiv:2607.24687].
  The proof linearizes the relevant concave Rényi functional at a comparison point dictated by the isotropic equality family. Schmidt-rank domination extends the equality geometry to an arbitrary anchor state, after which trace-distance duality and a noncommutative calibration estimate control the perturbation and anchor term without weakening the sharp constant. The latter estimate requires matrix analysis and is assisted by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol.

</details>


### [110] [Photon localization: a comparative study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04134)
*Laura M. Ferguson,Ricardo Rojas Castellano,Kayla Oltman,Joshua G. Fenwick,Rainer Dick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了二维高斯波包的光子局域化度量，发现所有度量在波包演化时间超过动量空间宽度倒数若干倍后趋于一致。其中Landau-Peierls波函数给出最大正偏移<r>-ct>0，而标量傅里叶变换给出最小偏移并最快收敛。通过离子阱模型探测器的散射矩阵推导局域探测概率，发现其可由三种局域化度量（Landau-Peierls波函数、能量波函数和Hawton密度）表达，且这三种度量在单光子波包时间演化中始终接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子局域化是量子光学中的基本问题，存在多种不同的数学定义（如Landau-Peierls波函数、能量波函数、Hawton密度等），但缺乏对它们之间关系的系统比较。本文旨在通过二维高斯波包统一比较这些度量，并通过实际探测模型检验哪种度量与物理可观测的局域探测概率真正对应。

Method: 采用二维高斯波包作为测试对象，数值比较多种光子局域化度量（包括Landau-Peierls波函数、标量傅里叶变换、能量波函数、Hawton密度等）的时间演化行为。同时构建由离子阱中离子组成的模型探测器，通过散射矩阵推导位置相关的光子探测概率，并将其与各局域化度量进行对比。

Result: 所有局域化度量在波包演化时间远大于动量空间宽度倒数时趋于一致。Landau-Peierls波函数产生最大正偏移<r>-ct>0，标量傅里叶变换产生最小偏移且收敛最快。局域探测概率可由Landau-Peierls波函数、能量波函数和Hawton密度三种度量表达，且这三者在整个时间演化过程中保持接近。

Conclusion: 光子的局域化探测概率并非唯一确定，而是可由多种局域化度量描述。Landau-Peierls、能量波函数和Hawton密度三种度量与实际离子阱探测器的散射矩阵结果一致且在演化中保持稳定，表明它们是描述光子局域探测的有效度量，而标量傅里叶变换则与实际探测偏差较大。

Abstract: We compare different measures for photon localization in terms of two-dimensional Gaussian wave packets. We find that all measures start to coalesce if the wave packet has evolved for times which are larger than a few times the inverse momentum-space width of the package. However, the Landau-Peierls wave function yields the largest positive offset <r>-ct>0 while the scalar Fourier transform of the wave packet yields the smallest offset and converges towards <r>-ct=0 fastest. We also discuss local detection of photons through a model detector consisting of ions in ion traps. The position-dependent detection probabilities are inferred from the scattering matrix. We find that the local detection probability for photons can be expressed in terms of three of the proposed localization measures, viz. the Landau-Peierls wave function, the energy wave function, and the Hawton density. Those three porposals also remain close throughout the time evolution of the single-photon wave packet.

</details>


### [111] [Diffusive Speed Limits for U(1)-Covariant Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04953)
*Jianqi Sheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维对称约束量子纠错编码器的制备时间下界问题，证明了U(1)电荷守恒与几何局域性共同导致的扩散输运限制，给出了精确的擦除律和编码时间下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速制备量子纠错编码对可扩展量子存储至关重要，但几何局域性和U(1)电荷守恒会施加不可避免的输运约束。理解这一约束的精确本质对于设计实际可行的量子编码协议具有重要意义。

Method: 结合精确互补通道几何、电荷扇区Haar分析和门分辨连通矩展开三种方法，研究一维协变编码器在标记擦除下的行为。对于局域数守恒砖墙电路，通过逻辑电荷扩散证明编码时间下界，并建立经典分量的混合上界和通道上界到源限制低支撑算符扩展问题的归约。

Result: 电荷-Haar编码达到相邻电荷下界的通用下界（仅差指数小修正），得到精确的n^{-1/2}大范围擦除律和锐利的半擦除相变。局域数守恒砖墙电路满足Ω(n^2)编码时间下界；证明经典分量的O(n^3)混合上界，并将完整通道上界归约为源限制低支撑算符扩展问题。

Conclusion: 这些结果识别出扩散是对称约束量子编码的操作性限制，为精确的成形时间建立了研究路径。

Abstract: Fast preparation of quantum error-correcting codes is essential for scalable quantum memories, but geometric locality and $U(1)$ charge conservation impose an unavoidable transport constraint. We combine exact complementary-channel geometry, charge-sector Haar analysis, and a gate-resolved connected-moment expansion to study one-dimensional covariant encoders under flagged erasure. Charge-Haar codes attain the universal adjacent-charge lower bound up to exponentially small corrections, yielding an exact $n^{-1/2}$ extensive-erasure law and a sharp half-erasure transition. For local number-conserving brickwork circuits, diffusion of the logical charge enforces an $Ω(n^2)$ encoding-time lower bound; we also prove an $O(n^3)$ mixing bound for the classical component and reduce the remaining full-channel upper bound to a source-restricted low-support operator-spreading problem. These results identify diffusion as an operational limit on symmetry-constrained quantum coding and establish a route to its exact formation time.

</details>


### [112] [Applications of Spin-Dependent Generalized Squeezing in Hybrid Spin-Oscillator Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04197)
*Kai Shinbrough,Donovan J. Webb,Iver R. Øvergaard,Oana Băzăvan,Sebastian Saner,Christopher J. Ballance,Raghavendra Srinivas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在混合自旋-谐振子量子处理器平台上，基于广义压缩相互作用提出并研究了四种新应用：几何相位门、N体自旋相互作用、振荡器测温以及高保真量子态制备，为混合平台的离散与连续变量量子计算和传感奠定基础。


<details>
