In driven Kerr resonators, the framework identifies preparations that suppress a switching mode, separates symmetry-resolved relaxation channels, and reveals bias-induced crossovers in projected multichannel routing while the coherent-preparation partition continues to deform. Preparation geometry and slow-sector propagation thus provide complementary operational information beyond Liouvillian eigenvalues alone.

</details>


### [149] [Perfect Games in Dimension-Bounded Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05092)
*E. Zambrini Cruzeiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究完美prepare-and-measure（准备-测量）游戏中量子与经典优势的精确刻画。对于禁止输出支撑约束，作者建立了两个结构性结果：二值输出支撑博弈可精确约化为冲突图的d-可着色性与d维正交表示问题；任意有限输出字母表下，完美qubit策略可被经典比特策略完全模拟。以13-射线qutrit图为例，构造了(13,8,2)的压缩博弈，得到C3=39<Q3=S=40，且8个Bob输入是该图二值输出实现的最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解prepare-and-measure博弈中量子优势的本质边界——即何时量子系统能实现经典系统无法实现的完美胜利。以往研究多关注概率性优势或特定实例，缺乏对完美"全有或全无"量子优势的通用结构性刻画，本文旨在通过图论工具系统回答这一问题。

Method: 将完美prepare-and-measure博弈的禁止输出支撑约束映射为冲突图问题：证明二值输出博弈中经典d级消息的完美可实现性等价于图d-着色，d维量子对应d维正交表示（OR）；利用edge biclique-cover数刻画最小Bob输入数。对多值输出情形，证明完美qubit策略可经典化（经典比特实现）。通过具体图（13-射线qutrit图、Torpedo图、反可区分性博弈）验证理论机制。

Result: （1）建立二值输出支撑博弈与冲突图之间的精确等价关系（经典⇄着色，量子⇄正交表示），并给出最小Bob输入数的组合刻画；（2）证明任意有限输出字母表下完美qubit策略可约化为经典比特策略；（3）构造13-射线qutrit图对应的压缩博弈(X,Y,B)=(13,8,2)，实现C3=39<Q3=S=40的量子优势，且8是最小Bob输入数；（4）图扩展方法证明该机制在任意维度成立。

Conclusion: 完美prepare-and-measure博弈的量子优势可通过图论结构（冲突图、正交表示、biclique-cover）得到精确刻画，连通了精确通信、图着色、语境性、状态排除与零错误信息论等多个领域。二值输出情形完全归结为经典图着色与量子正交表示的对比；而qubit情形下经典比特策略已足够强大，说明量子优势需要更高维系统或更复杂输出结构才能展现。

Abstract: Perfect prepare-and-measure games exhibit an all-or-nothing quantum advantage: a quantum system of dimension $d$ satisfies every prescribed winning constraint, whereas a classical $d$-level message cannot. We establish two structural results for such forbidden-output support constraints. First, every binary-output support game reduces exactly to a conflict graph: perfect classical realization with a $d$-level message is equivalent to $d$-colorability, perfect $d$-dimensional quantum realization is equivalent to a $d$-dimensional orthogonal representation, and the minimum number of Bob inputs realizing a fixed conflict graph is its edge biclique-cover number. Second, for an arbitrary finite output alphabet, every perfect qubit strategy admits a perfect classical-bit realization. As a flagship application, the $13$-ray qutrit graph yields a compressed game $(X,Y,B)=(13,8,2)$ with $C_3=39<Q_3=S=40$, and eight Bob inputs are minimal among all binary-output realizations of that graph. Graph extensions demonstrate the mechanism in every dimension, while Torpedo and antidistinguishability games illustrate the genuinely nonbinary regime. These results connect exact communication, graph coloring, contextuality, state exclusion, and zero-error information theory.

</details>


### [150] [Imaginarity as a necessary resource for trainability in QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05093)
*Syed Muhammad Ali Hassan,Kostas Blekos,Stefan Kühn,Nikos Kollas,Karl Jansen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明QAOA中用于调优电路最终参数的梯度受"虚部量度"（imaginarity）约束，该量度按电路连接强度与问题评分差异对候选解之间的相位关系进行加权。虚部量度是梯度非零的必要但非充分条件，并将该界扩展到三种常见噪声模型，在Max-Cut模拟中与梯度进行了数值比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QAOA优化中的梯度行为对于改进其收敛性和性能至关重要，但此前缺乏对最终参数梯度大小决定性因素的清晰刻画。

Method: 通过数学推导证明QAOA最终参数梯度被虚部量度（imaginarity）所界定，并将该证明推广到三种常见噪声模型；在Max-Cut问题上进行数值模拟以比较该界与实际梯度。

Result: 建立了梯度与虚部量度之间的理论边界关系，验证了虚部量度为梯度非零的必要条件（非充分），并在噪声环境下保持该界的有效性，Max-Cut数值实验证实了理论结果。

Conclusion: 虚部量度是QAOA梯度存在性的关键量，为理解和设计QAOA参数优化策略提供了新的理论视角，且该结论在噪声环境下仍然成立。

Abstract: The quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) tackles combinatorial problems by tuning a quantum circuit in a classical loop, often guided by gradients. We show that the gradient used to tune the circuit's final parameter is bounded by imaginarity, which weights phase relationships between candidate solutions by how strongly the circuit connects them and how differently the problem scores them. Imaginarity is necessary but not sufficient for a nonzero gradient. We extend the bound to three common noise models and compare it numerically with the gradient in Max-Cut simulations.

</details>


### [151] [Representational separation between unitary and channel quantum generative models via shared classical randomness at shallow depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05110)
*Arunava Majumder,Marius Krumm,Hendrik Poulsen Nautrup,Hans J. Briegel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在固定浅层深度下，共享经典随机性足以使有界连通性的量子信道生成模型（augmented shallow unitary Born models）比纯酉浅层模型具备严格可扩展的表征分离，并进一步展示了测量式量子计算（MBQC）可作为实现这种共享经典随机性的自然方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的量子硬件限制了电路深度和几何局域连通性，从而限制了浅层酉Born模型所能表达的输出分布。尽管已有研究表明在随机性引入可提升经验生成性能，且在受限小规模架构下被证明能表征严格更大的分布族，但固定浅层深度下对任意大规模系统是否成立此前仍然开放。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者将有界连通性的浅层酉电路（后接计算基测量）与空间分离的局域Pauli操作相结合，这些操作的联合施加由一个经典采样的随机比特控制。通过分析由此产生的浅层信道模型对经典输出分布中长程相关的生成能力，并与纯酉浅层有界连通模型进行对比；同时利用测量式量子计算（MBQC）通过对随机测量结果的适应性调整来实现所需的共享经典随机性，并在MBQC基生成模型上进行数值实验验证。

Result: 证明共享经典随机性足以建立严格可扩展的表征分离：浅层信道模型能生成长程相关分布，而任何纯酉浅层有界连通模型均无法再现。在一维最近邻架构下，纯酉模型在最优情况下需要深度Ω(N)才能产生此类分布。数值实验进一步支持了理论分析结论。

Conclusion: 共享经典随机性这一在纠缠理论中相对较弱的资源，在固定浅层深度下足以为大规模系统提供严格的表征优势，且MBQC为实现该共享随机性提供了自然的物理实现途径。这为浅层量子生成模型的表达能力提供了新的理论洞见。

Abstract: Near-term quantum hardware limits circuit depth and often imposes geometrically local connectivity for quantum generative models, restricting the output distributions accessible to shallow unitary Born models. Introducing stochasticity into a unitary quantum Born model can improve the empirical generative performance of the resulting channel model and, for a restricted small-scale architecture, has been proven to represent a strictly larger family of distributions than its unitary counterpart. However, whether such randomness provides a provable separation at fixed shallow depth for arbitrarily large systems has remained open. Here, we show that shared classical randomness, a comparatively weak resource from entanglement theory, is sufficient to establish such a strict scalable representational separation over the corresponding shallow unitary Born model. More specifically, we augment bounded-connectivity shallow unitary circuits, followed by computational-basis measurements, with spatially separated local Pauli operations, whose joint application is controlled by a single classically sampled random bit. The resulting shallow-depth channel model generates long-range correlations in the classical output distribution that no purely unitary shallow-depth model with bounded connectivity can reproduce. For one-dimensional nearest-neighbour architectures, reproducing such distributions with a purely unitary model can require depth $Ω(N)$ in the worst case. We further show that measurement-based quantum computation (MBQC) provides a natural implementation of the required shared classical randomness through suitable adaptation of the random measurement outcomes. Numerical experiments on MBQC-based generative models support the analytical results.

</details>


### [152] [Fast Quantum Interconnects via Neutral Atom Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05147)
*Sina Zeytinoglu,Wenchao Xu,Thomas Pohl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的量子互连方案，利用里德堡原子间的强偶极-偶极相互作用，在无光学腔的情况下实现静态量子比特与传播光子之间的纠缠生成，纠缠生成速率可达3×10^5 s⁻¹，接近中性原子量子处理器双量子比特门的速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在远距离量子比特之间分发纠缠是可扩展量子计算的关键要素，然而现有方法在速率和可扩展性方面存在瓶颈。作者旨在设计一种无需光学腔、可与现有中性原子量子处理器兼容的高速可扩展量子互连方案。

Method: 利用原子里德堡态之间的强偶极-偶极相互作用，在静止量子比特与传播光子之间直接产生纠缠，规避了对光学腔的依赖；针对实际实验参数给出了最优纠缠生成协议的详细条件描述。

Result: 在镱原子里德堡态条件下，纠缠生成速率可达到不低于3×10^5 s⁻¹的量级，与当前中性原子量子处理器双量子比特门的速率水平相当。

Conclusion: 该量子互连方案具有内在的可扩展性和设计灵活性，为基于中性原子量子架构的分布式量子网络提供了一条有前景的实现路径。

Abstract: Distributing entanglement between distant qubits is a crucial element of scalable quantum computing. Here, we describe a scalable quantum interconnect that generates remote entanglement at rates approaching those compatible with two-qubit gates of current neutral-atom quantum processors. The proposed approach exploits the strong dipole-dipole interactions between atomic Rydberg states to generate entanglement between stationary qubits and propagating photons, without the need for an optical cavity. We provide a thorough description of the optimal conditions for the developed entanglement-generation protocol for realistic experimental parameters and demonstrate that entanglement-generation rates $\gtrsim 3\times 10^5$ s$^{-1}$ can be achieved using Rydberg states of ytterbium atoms. Given the inherent scalability and design flexibility of the proposed interconnect, our results suggest a promising approach towards distributed networks based on neutral-atom quantum architectures.

</details>

### [153] [Explicit Hamiltonian Classification in the $F_4(0)$ Toric Degeneration of $CP^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04535)
*Han Lou,Shuo Zhang*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 该论文给出了平滑化F_4(0)环面退化中正则拉格朗日环面纤维的哈密顿同痕类的显式坐标描述，使用CP²(√2)上的Oakley-Usher坐标，并确定了壁纤维与非壁纤维各自在哈密顿同痕意义下的归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究辛几何中退化簇的平滑化上的拉格朗日环面纤维的哈密顿同痕分类，特别是在环面退化F_4(0)的平滑化上，理解不同区域的纤维（壁与非壁）在哈密顿同痕意义下的关系。

Method: 利用Oakley-Usher在CP²(√2)上给出的显式坐标，对平滑化F_4(0)的正则拉格朗日环面纤维进行坐标描述，并系统分类壁纤维与非壁纤维的哈密顿同痕类。

Result: 壁纤维不与任何标准环面纤维哈密顿同痕，且任意两个不同的壁纤维之间也互不哈密顿同痕；对于非壁纤维，找到了它们各自哈密顿同痕等价的标准环面纤维。

Conclusion: 通过对F_4(0)平滑化上拉格朗日环面纤维的显式坐标分析，完整刻画了其哈密顿同痕类：壁纤维形成互不相同且非标准的同痕类，而非壁纤维均可对应到标准环面纤维。

Abstract: We give an explicit coordinate description of the Hamiltonian isotopy classes of the regular Lagrangian torus fibers of the smoothing \(\widehat{F}_4(0)\) of the \(F_4(0)\) toric degeneration, expressed in the explicit Oakley--Usher coordinates on \(\CP^2(\sqrt2)\). For the wall fibers, they are not Hamiltonian isotopic to standard toric fibers, and no two distinct wall fibers are Hamiltonian isotopic. For the off-wall fibers, we find the standard toric fibers they are Hamiltonian isotopic to.

</details>


### [154] [Resonances and string point invertibility for compact rank one symmetric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04691)
*Kai Cieliebak,Alexandru Oancea,Egor Shelukhin*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文计算了秩一紧对称空间的Rabinowitz环路同调的BV代数结构，并据此证明了该空间满足"弦点可逆性"当且仅当其欧拉示性数等于环路同调系数域的特征。这蕴含了Viterbo猜想在余切盘丛中的某些情形。此外，弦点可逆的秩一紧对称空间在任意黎曼度量下，其环路同调类的临界级满足关于度数的共振条件及闭测地线的稠密条件，推广了Hingston和Rademacher关于球面的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过计算Rabinowitz环路同调的BV代数结构来研究秩一紧对称空间的拓扑与几何性质，进而验证Viterbo猜想在特定情形下的成立，并将球面上的经典结果推广到更广泛的秩一紧对称空间类别。

Method: 作者计算秩一紧对称空间的Rabinowitz环路同调的Batalin-Vilkovisky代数结构，利用该代数结构判定弦点可逆性（string point invertibility），并通过这一同调条件推导Viterbo猜想的若干情形，同时借助临界级分析建立共振条件与闭测地线的密度条件。

Result: 证明了秩一紧对称空间满足弦点可逆性当且仅当其欧拉示数等于环路同调系数域的特征；验证了Viterbo猜想关于谱范数一致界的若干情形；并证明了弦点可逆时环路同调类的临界级满足共振条件和闭测地线密度条件，将Hingston和Rademacher关于球面的结果推广到更广的情形。

Conclusion: Rabinowitz环路同调的BV代数结构为研究秩一紧对称空间的弦点可逆性提供了有效工具，由此可推出Viterbo猜想的部分情形，并在闭测地线理论中获得共振与密度结论，扩展了既有结果的应用范围。

Abstract: We calculate the Batalin-Vilkovisky (BV) algebra structure of Rabinowitz loop homology for compact rank one symmetric spaces. As a consequence, we prove that such a space satisfies a natural homological condition called string point invertibility if and only if its Euler characteristic is equal to the characteristic of the coefficient field for loop homology. This implies certain cases of Viterbo's conjecture on a uniform bound on the spectral norm of exact Lagrangian submanifolds in cotangent disk bundles. Furthermore, we prove that whenever a compact rank one symmetric space is string point invertible, the critical levels of its loop homology classes with respect to an arbitrary Riemannian metric satisfy a resonance condition with respect to degrees and a density condition for closed geodesics. This generalizes results of Hingston and Rademacher for spheres to a broader class of compact rank one symmetric spaces.

</details>
